**版本**：v2.2 Official Half-2 Tournament Specification（经双红队审计冻结）  \n4: > **赛制核心**：全员**继承本人上半场 `submission.py` 与本人战报**，限时 **40 分钟**同时重构 `build_dungeon()`（逻辑迷宫建造）与 `decide_action(state, memory)`（全图明牌全局状态空间求解除），进行全交叉 `mode == \"FULL\"` 对抗。\n5: \n6: ---\n7: \n8: ## 🔥 零、 v2.1 → v2.2 核心变更速查表（必读！）\n9: \n10: | 维度 | 上半场 v2.1 (`BLIND`) | 下半场 v2.2 (`FULL` 全图明牌双重构) |\n11: | :--- | :--- | :--- |\n12: | **视野模式** | 局部 37 格圆形迷雾 + LOS | **全图永久明牌（`mode == \"FULL\"`）**：第 1 回合起 `visible_mask` 全为 `True`（仅 `HEAL`/`MIMIC` 仍脱敏显示为 `CHEST`，全图 `clues` 直接可见） |\n13: | **比赛时长与任务** | 60 分钟，单写盲视算法与盲战图 | **40 分钟，攻守双重构**：用 1 份 `submission.py` 同时升级造图（防明牌）与破阵（明牌全局搜索） |\n14: | **决策调用时限** | 每步 `0.5 秒` | **Turn 1 开放 `2.0 秒`** 全局规划预处理；**Turn 2..240 仍为 `0.5 秒`**（注意自我限时 `< 1.6s` 防超时！） |\n15: | **超时容错与判负** | 超时/异常 3 连振判 `DEAD`（算对手实杀） | 单次超时（`streak < 3`）记 `WAIT` 并自动重启干净工作进程；连错 3 次判 **`DQ` 弃权**（不计入对手实杀数） |\n16: | **新机制 ①：压板门** | 无 | **`PLATE`**（45G，上限 4，防爆免疫，**仅 `BOX` 可压住**）+ **`P_GATE`**（30G，上限 8，防爆免疫）+ **`BOX` 上限 `4 → 6`** |\n17: | **新机制 ②：蓝双拍炮** | 仅红炮 `LASER_*` (`turn % 3 == 0`) | 新增 **`LASER2_U/D/L/R`**（90G，与红炮共享 `12` 门上限，可炸毁，**`turn % 4 == 0`** 发射）→ **12 拍双频节奏**！ |\n18: | **新机制 ③：单向门** | 无 | 新增 **`ONEWAY_U/D/L/R`**（30G，上限 12，防爆免疫）：勇者与箱子仅可顺箭头方向进入；激光与空中飞石全向穿透 |\n19: | **出生点公平准入** | 仅 `3×3` 出生区 `EMPTY` | 新增 **出生 4 回合准入校验**：至少存在 `2` 个不同 Turn-1 无伤坐标，且各自存在 0 机关伤活过 **Turn 4** 的序列 |\n20: | **`author_solution` 上限** | `<= 240` 步 | 收紧至 **`<= 200` 步**（实战仍打满 `240` 回合）；`static_geometry_reachable` 仅作警告，**精确自证通过即放行** |\n21: | **失守通关计分** | `2.0*D + 120*(T-1)/239` | **`1.7*D + 150*(T-1)/239`**（上限锁死 `320.0 == TIMEOUT 底分`，金库前等待净收益为 0） |\n22: \n23: ---\n24: \n25: ## 一、 比赛纪律与运行环境约束\n26: \n27: 1. **单文件交付**：在考场目录保存最终 **`submission.py`**，同时实现 `build_dungeon() -> dict` 与 `decide_action(state: dict, memory: dict) -> str`。40:00 整点以最后一次合法保存的 `submission.py` 为准。\n28: 2. **禁止与允许的库**：\n29:    - 运行环境：CPython 3.11+。\n30:    - **自由使用**纯计算/数据结构/科学计算标准库（`math`, `heapq`, `collections`, `queue`, `array`, `itertools`, `copy`, `random`, `time`, `json`, `numpy`, `networkx`, `sympy`, `mpmath` 等）及常规类私有属性（`self._cache`）。\n31:    - **严禁**任何文件 I/O（`open`, `os`, `pathlib`, `io`, `np.load*`）、网络通信、进程控制及动态反射（`eval`, `exec`, `getattr`, `setattr`, `delattr`, `inspect`, `pickle`, `ctypes`, `__class__`/`__globals__` 等），**违者直接 0 分废标**。\n32:    - **严禁联网检索**：禁止任何 Web Search / Fetch / 查询外部资料。\n33: 3. **进程级隔离与超时恢复**：\n34:    - 正式评测每场对局独立运行在子进程沙盒，初始 `memory = {}`，固定种子 `random.seed(20261115)`。\n35:    - `import <= 1.5s`，`build_dungeon() <= 2.0s`，`decide_action()` **Turn 1 `<= 2.0s` / Turn 2..240 `<= 0.5s`**。\n36:    - 若某回合超时、`raise` 异常或返回非 14 种合法动作，本回合强制按 `\"WAIT\"` 执行，`error_streak += 1`；若发生超时且 `error_streak < 3`，裁判将在下回合前**自动重建工作进程**（注意：`memory` 会清空，请做好无 `memory` 时的 Turn>1 快速重建或轻量兜底！）；`error_streak == 3` 时判本局 **`DQ`（接口三振弃权）**。\n37: \n38: ---\n39: \n40: ## 二、 赛场空间、组件经济表与建图校验\n41: \n42: ### 1. 网格、地标与出生点防秒杀准入\n43: - 画布 `40 × 26`（$x \\in [0,39], y \\in [0,25]$）。最外圈 128 格必须全 `\"T_W\"`（`0G`，不占 120 格配额）。\n44: - 出生点 `S = (2, 13)`，净空区 $x \\in [1,3], y \\in [12,14]$ 必须全 `\"EMPTY\"`。\n45: - 地标各恰 1 个（`0G`）：`K1` $x \\in [12,34], y \\in [2,10]$；`K2` $x \\in [12,34], y \\in [15,23]$；$|x_{K1}-x_{K2}|+|y_{K1}-y_{K2}| \\ge 12$；`V` $x \\in [33,37], y \\in [4,21]$。\n46: - **v2.2 新增【出生 4 回合准入规则】**：禁止构造 Turn 1–4 出生点强制吃伤或“开局第一步单选题”。必须存在至少 **`2` 个不同的第 1 回合末坐标 `(hx, hy)`** 满足零机关伤害存活，且各自至少存在一条第 2→3→4 回合零机关伤害安全行动序（防 Turn 3 红炮 / Turn 4 蓝炮开局强杀）。\n47: - **`static_geometry_reachable()` 降级提示**：不再因“无开关可炸红门”或“两格深渊+箱子搭桥”误杀合法巧思地图；**以 `author_solution`（`1..200` 步，恰好最后一步存活踏上 `V`）作为唯一可达性硬证书**。\n48: \n49: ### 2. 魔王施工组件参数表（总预算上限 `3,000 G`）\n50: \n51: | 符号 (`grid[y][x]`) | 名称 | 单价 | 单图上限 | 防爆 | 核心机制 |\n52: | :--- | :--- | :---: | :---: | :---: | :--- |\n53: | `\"EMPTY\"` | 安全平地 | `0G` | 无上限 | — | 普通摩擦地面。 |\n54: | `\"K1\"`, `\"K2\"`, `\"V\"` | 双钥匙 / 金库 | `0G` | 各 1 | **免疫** | 先拿齐 `K1`/`K2` 再进 `V` 立刻通关。 |\n55: | `\"W\"` | 普通砖墙 | `5G` | 无上限 | 可炸 | 高墙阻挡。 |\n56: | `\"T_W\"` | 钛金承重墙 | `25G` | `120` *(不含外框)* | **免疫** | 不可摧毁的高墙。 |\n57: | `\"ICE\"` | 溜冰滑道 | `10G` | 无上限 | 可炸 | 同回合沿当前方向连续滑行。 |\n58: | `\"MIRROR_NE/SE/SW/NW\"` | 90°折跃斜坡 | `25G` | 合计 `30` | 可炸 | 折转 `dir` 并继续向 `new_dir` 滑出 1 格。 |\n59: | `\"SPRING_U/D/L/R\"` | 强力弹簧 | `35G` | 合计 `20` | 可炸 | 强制转向并向朝向腾空弹射最多 3 格。 |\n60: | `\"PIT\"` | 致命深渊 | `40G` | 无上限 | **免疫** | 勇者踏入 `-100 HP`；`BOX` 填坑化为 `EMPTY`。 |\n61: | `\"CRACK\"` | 脆冰断桥 | `20G` | `40` | 变 `PIT` | 勇者或箱子**离开瞬间**（且仅在位移实际发生时）变为 `PIT`。 |\n62: | `\"SWITCH\"` | 红蓝反相开关 | `30G` | `6` | 可炸 | 勇者/`BOX`/飞石触发 `gates_flipped = not gates_flipped`。 |\n63: | `\"R_GATE\"`, `\"B_GATE\"` | 红门 / 蓝门 | `20G` | 合计 `16` | 可炸 | `gates_flipped == False` 时 `R_GATE` 关、`B_GATE` 开。 |\n64: | **`\"PLATE\"`** *(NEW)* | **金色压板** | **`45G`** | **`4`** | **免疫** | 地形层。**仅 `occupant == \"BOX\"` 可激活**（勇者与定时炸弹均不触发！）。 |\n65: | **`\"P_GATE\"`** *(NEW)* | **金色压板门** | **`30G`** | **`8`** | **免疫** | 仅当 `plates_active == True`（存在至少 1 个压板且全被 `BOX` 压住）时开启，否则为高墙。**硬约束：若使用 `P_GATE`，必须满足 `1 <= PLATE <= BOX`**！ |\n66: | **`\"LASER_U/D/L/R\"`** | **红频激光炮** | `75G` | **红+蓝合计 `12`** | 可炸 | 高墙炮座，**`turn % 3 == 0`**（3,6,9,12…）发射 10 格射线，`-50 HP` / 引爆 TNT。 |\n67: | **`\"LASER2_U/D/L/R\"`** *(NEW)* | **蓝高频激光炮** | **`90G`** | **与红炮共享 `12`** | 可炸 | 高墙炮座，**`turn % 4 == 0`**（4,8,12,16…）发射 10 格射线，`-50 HP` / 引爆 TNT。第 `12k` 拍红蓝同帧齐射！ |\n68: | **`\"ONEWAY_U/D/L/R\"`** *(NEW)* | **单向通行门** | **`30G`** | **合计 `12`** | **免疫** | 实体（勇者/`BOX`）**仅可沿箭头方向进入**该格（不限离开方向）。激光与空中飞石自由穿透。 |\n69: | `\"TNT\"` | 连锁火药桶 | `40G` | `12` | 爆 | 占用层阻挡；3×3 造成 `-60 HP` 并摧毁非免疫格。 |\n70: | `\"BOX\"` | 重型合金箱 | `30G` | **`6`** *(↑4)* | 可炸 | 占用层；可推动、填坑、压 `PLATE`、挡激光、挡飞石、撞触发 `TNT`。 |\n71: | `\"HEAL\"` / `\"MIMIC\"` | 真/假宝箱 | `30G`/`60G` | 各 `4` | 可炸 | 明牌赛均脱敏显示 `CHEST`，但 Turn 1 全图 `clues` 已直接可见，可通过孤立线索 100% 辨别真假！ |\n72: | `\"PORTAL_1A/1B/2A/2B\"` | 双子传送门 | `80G/对` | `2 对` | **免疫** | 必须成对；每回合至多传送 2 次。 |\n73: \n74: ---\n75: \n76: ## 三、 v2.2 物理引擎与新机关精确判定语义\n77: \n78: ### 1. `HIGH_WALL` 与实时门态\n79: - **`is_plates_active()`（纯实时派生，永不陈旧）**：当且仅当地图存在至少 1 个 `PLATE`，且所有 `PLATE` 上均有 `occupant == \"BOX\"` 时返回 `True`。无论是在推箱子子步（Step 3 变更 `occupant`）、还是阶段 C/D 爆炸炸毁或填坑消耗 `BOX` 后，`plates_active` 立刻重新计算生效。\n80: - **`HIGH_WALL` 集合**：`W`, `T_W`, 全部 8 种 `LASER_* / LASER2_*` 炮座，关闭状态的 `R_GATE` / `B_GATE`，以及当 `not plates_active` 时的 `P_GATE`。\n81: - **闭门滞留规则**：若实体停留在开着的门内时开关翻转或压板松开导致关门，实体不受挤压伤害：勇者仍可在原地停留或主动走向外侧合法格离开；**被困在关闭门格内的 `BOX` 保留占用，但从门外推动它需要先开门（因外侧进门方向受高墙阻挡）**。\n82: \n83: ### 2. `ONEWAY_U/D/L/R` 全场景统一裁决\n84: 记 `blocks_oneway(x, y, dir)` 为真，当且仅当 `terrain[y][x] == \"ONEWAY_<d>\"` 且本次进入方向向量 `dir != DIR_VECTORS[d]`：\n85: 1. **普通 `MOVE_*` 与推箱**：前置先验 `(x1, y1)`（若该格上停着一个 `BOX`，逆向推动会直接整次拒绝，**箱子绝不移动且脚下 `CRACK` 不坍塌**！）；若推箱子，`BOX` 目标格 `p2` 验 `dir`，箱链完成后勇者跟进 `p1` 再验 `dir`。\n86: 2. **主动 `JUMP_*`**：中间飞越格 `p1` **与** 落点格 `p2` 若均不阻挡（非高墙且均不 `blocks_oneway(..., jump_dir)`）方可执行（受阻失败仍扣 1 次跳跃，但脚下 `CRACK` 不坍塌）。\n87: 3. **冰面与折跃斜坡**：`ICE` 按当前 `dir` 检查下一格；**`MIRROR_*` 先折转得到 `new_dir`，再用 `new_dir` 检查目标格**。\n88: 4. **弹簧 `SPRING_*`**：沿 `sdir` 逐格检测 $k=1,2,3$；若在第 $k$ 格遇逆向 `ONEWAY_*` 则视作阻挡停于 `k-1`（$k=1$ 时留在弹簧原地）。\n89: 5. **传送门 `PORTAL_*`**：从 A 传送到 B 格本身不验向量；到达配对端 B 后保持原方向 `dir` 尝试向前迈出 1 格时，若前方格为逆向 `ONEWAY_*` 则被拦住并停稳在配对端 B 上。\n90: 6. **飞石 `THROW_*`**：**空中 6 格飞行自由穿过所有方向的 `ONEWAY_*`**（正常消耗射程）；若第 6 格落入 `ICE`/`MIRROR_*` 转入地面滑行，滑行撞到 `ONEWAY_*` 时按普通非冰地面立即停稳消失。\n91: 7. **激光**：`LASER_*` 与 `LASER2_*` 光束自由穿过所有方向的 `ONEWAY_*`（但仍被站在 `ONEWAY_*` 上的 `BOX` 遮挡）。\n92: \n93: ### 3. 双频激光（阶段 D & `laser_warnings`）\n94: - 仅当 **`turn % 3 == 0 or turn % 4 == 0`** 时进入阶段 D：\n95:   - `turn % 3 == 0`：所有存活红炮 `LASER_*`（`period=3`）开火；\n96:   - `turn % 4 == 0`：所有存活蓝炮 `LASER2_*`（`period=4`）开火；\n97:   - **`turn % 12 == 0`**：红炮与蓝炮**基于阶段 C 结束后的同一快照同步齐射**，叠加命中不同炮座分别计 `50` 伤（被 2 门以上命中即 `-100 HP` 瞬间暴毙！），同时击中同一 `TNT` 仅在该批爆炸闭包引爆一次，不撤销本拍已发射激光。\n98: - **`state[\"laser_warnings\"]`**：当回合会发射激光的回合返回当前阶段 A 前快照下所有可见预警射线格：`{\"x\": bx, \"y\": by, \"dir\": \"U/D/L/R\", \"period\": 3|4, \"cannon\": [lx, ly]}`（按 `(y, x, dir, period, cannon)` 排序）。\n99: \n100: ---\n101: \n102: ## 四、 计分公式与接口 Schema\n103: \n104: - **勇者分 `Score_Hero`（同 v2.1）**：\n105:   - 通关：$600 + 1.5 \\times HP_{\\text{left}} + 150 \\times \\frac{240 - T}{239} + 100 \\times g$\n106:   - 失败/超时/DQ：$200 \\times k + 200 \\times r$\n107: - **魔王分 `Score_Demon`**：\n108:   - **实杀 `DEAD`**：$500 + 50 \\times (3 - P) + D + 250 \\times g$\n109:   - **困死 `TIMEOUT` 或对方接口弃权 `DQ`**：$320 + 50 \\times (3 - P) + D + 125 \\times g$\n110:   - **金库失守 `CLEARED`（v2.2 零和微调）**：$1.7 \\times D + 150 \\times \\frac{T - 1}{239} \\in [0, 320.0]$\n111: - **`decide_action(state, memory)` 输入新增字段**：\n112:   - `state[\"mode\"]` 恒为 `\"FULL\"`；\n113:   - `state[\"plates_active\"]`：`bool`，当前全场压板门是否全部开启；\n114:   - `state[\"laser_warnings\"]`：每项包含 `\"x\", \"y\", \"dir\", \"period\", \"cannon\"`。"
      }
    ]
  }
}

= set(self.exposed_cells)\n        c.stage = self.stage\n        c.stage_d0 = self.stage_d0\n        c.stage_dmin = self.stage_dmin\n        return c\n\n    def is_plates_active(self) -> bool:\n        if not self.plates:\n            return False\n        for px, py in self.plates:\n            if self.occupant[py][px] != \"BOX\":\n                return False\n        return True\n\n    def is_high_wall(self, x: int, y: int) -> bool:\n        if not in_bounds(x, y):\n            return True\n        t = self.terrain[y][x]\n        if t in (\"W\", \"T_W\") or t in ALL_LASER_DIR:\n            return True\n        if t == \"R_GATE\" and not self.gates_flipped:\n            return True\n        if t == \"B_GATE\" and self.gates_flipped:\n            return True\n        if t == \"P_GATE\" and not self.is_plates_active():\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    def blocks_oneway(self, x: int, y: int, dname: str) -> bool:\n        if not in_bounds(x, y):\n            return False\n        t = self.terrain[y][x]\n        if t in ONEWAY_DIR:\n            return ONEWAY_DIR[t] != dname\n        return False\n\n    def _dist_to_next_target(self, pos):\n        k1 = tuple(self.targets[\"K1\"])\n        k2 = tuple(self.targets[\"K2\"])\n        v = tuple(self.targets[\"V\"])\n        if not self.has_k1 and not self.has_k2:\n            return min(manhattan(pos, k1), manhattan(pos, k2))\n        elif not self.has_k1:\n            return manhattan(pos, k1)\n        elif not self.has_k2:\n            return manhattan(pos, k2)\n        else:\n            return manhattan(pos, v)\n\n    def _on_hero_arrive_progress(self, pos, stage_changed: bool):\n        if stage_changed:\n            self.stage = int(self.has_k1) + int(self.has_k2)\n            self.stage_d0 = self._dist_to_next_target(pos)\n            self.stage_dmin = self.stage_d0\n        else:\n            d = self._dist_to_next_target(pos)\n            if d < self.stage_dmin:\n                self.stage_dmin = d\n\n    def _clear_mimic(self, x: int, y: int):\n        self.terrain[y][x] = \"EMPTY\"\n        clue_pos = self.mimic_to_clue.pop((x, y), None)\n        if clue_pos is not None:\n            self.clues[clue_pos[1]][clue_pos[0]] = False\n\n    def _can_hero_enter(self, x: int, y: int, dname: str) -> bool:\n        if self.is_high_wall(x, y) or self.blocks_oneway(x, y, dname):\n            return False\n        if self.occupant[y][x] in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    def _can_box_enter(self, x: int, y: int, dname: str) -> bool:\n        if self.is_high_wall(x, y) or self.blocks_oneway(x, y, dname):\n            return False\n        if self.occupant[y][x] is not None:\n            return False\n        if (x, y) == (self.hx, self.hy):\n            return False\n        if self.terrain[y][x] in (\"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\"):\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    def _find_portal_pair(self, portal_tile: str):\n        pair_tile = PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[portal_tile]\n        if pair_tile in self.portals:\n            return self.portals[pair_tile]\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT):\n            for x in range(GRID_WIDTH):\n                if self.terrain[y][x] == pair_tile:\n                    self.portals[pair_tile] = (x, y)\n                    return (x, y)\n        return None\n\n    def _displace(self, entity: str, p0, p1, dname: str, state_ctx: dict, suppress_portal_once: bool = False):\n        state_ctx[\"steps_taken\"] += 1\n        x0, y0 = p0\n        x1, y1 = p1\n\n        if self.terrain[y0][x0] == \"CRACK\":\n            self.terrain[y0][x0] = \"PIT\"\n\n        if entity == \"BOX\":\n            self.occupant[y0][x0] = None\n            self.occupant[y1][x1] = \"BOX\"\n        else:\n            self.hx, self.hy = x1, y1\n            self.exposed_cells.add((x1, y1))\n\n        t1 = self.terrain[y1][x1]\n        stop_momentum = False\n        if entity == \"BOX\":\n            if t1 == \"PIT\":\n                self.occupant[y1][x1] = None\n                self.terrain[y1][x1] = \"EMPTY\"\n                return\n            elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n                self._clear_mimic(x1, y1)\n            elif t1 == \"SWITCH\":\n                self.gates_flipped = not self.gates_flipped\n        else:\n            if t1 == \"PIT\":\n                dmg = min(self.hp, 100)\n                self.trap_damage += dmg\n                self.hp -= 100\n            elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n                dmg = min(self.hp, 45)\n                self.trap_damage += dmg\n                self.hp -= 45\n                self._clear_mimic(x1, y1)\n                stop_momentum = True\n            elif t1 == \"HEAL\":\n                self.hp = min(100, self.hp + 30)\n                self.terrain[y1][x1] = \"EMPTY\"\n            elif t1 == \"SWITCH\":\n                self.gates_flipped = not self.gates_flipped\n\n        if entity == \"HERO\" and self.hp <= 0:\n            self._on_hero_arrive_progress((x1, y1), False)\n            self.status = \"DEAD\"\n            return\n\n        if entity == \"HERO\":\n            stage_changed = False\n            cur_t = self.terrain[y1][x1]\n            if cur_t == \"K1\":\n                self.has_k1 = True\n                self.terrain[y1][x1] = \"EMPTY\"\n                stage_changed = True\n            elif cur_t == \"K2\":\n                self.has_k2 = True\n                self.terrain[y1][x1] = \"EMPTY\"\n                stage_changed = True\n            self._on_hero_arrive_progress((x1, y1), stage_changed)\n            if cur_t == \"V\" and self.has_k1 and self.has_k2 and self.hp > 0:\n                self.status = \"CLEARED\"\n                return\n\n        if state_ctx[\"steps_taken\"] >= 32 or stop_momentum:\n            return\n\n        cur_t = self.terrain[y1][x1]\n        can_enter = self._can_hero_enter if entity == \"HERO\" else self._can_box_enter\n\n        if suppress_portal_once:\n            if state_ctx[\"portal_count\"] >= 2:\n                return\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n            nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n            if can_enter(nx, ny, dname):\n                self._displace(entity, (x1, y1), (nx, ny), dname, state_ctx, False)\n            return\n\n        if cur_t == \"ICE\":\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n            nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n            if can_enter(nx, ny, dname):\n                self._displace(entity, (x1, y1), (nx, ny), dname, state_ctx, False)\n        elif cur_t in MIRROR_MAP:\n            ndir = MIRROR_MAP[cur_t][dname]\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[ndir]\n            nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n            if can_enter(nx, ny, ndir):\n                self._displace(entity, (x1, y1), (nx, ny), ndir, state_ctx, False)\n        elif cur_t in SPRING_DIR:\n            sdir = SPRING_DIR[cur_t]\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[sdir]\n            blocked_k = None\n            for k in (1, 2, 3):\n                kx, ky = x1 + k * dx, y1 + k * dy\n                is_blk = False\n                if not in_bounds(kx, ky) or self.is_high_wall(kx, ky) or self.blocks_oneway(kx, ky, sdir):\n                    is_blk = True\n                elif self.occupant[ky][kx] in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                    is_blk = True\n                elif entity == \"BOX\" and ((kx, ky) == (self.hx, self.hy) or self.terrain[ky][kx] in (\"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\")):\n                    is_blk = True\n                if is_blk:\n                    blocked_k = k\n                    if entity == \"BOX\" and in_bounds(kx, ky) and self.occupant[ky][kx] == \"TNT\":\n                        state_ctx[\"tnt_armed\"].add((kx, ky))\n                    break\n            if blocked_k == 1:\n                return\n            tk = (blocked_k - 1) if blocked_k in (2, 3) else 3\n            self._displace(entity, (x1, y1), (x1 + tk * dx, y1 + tk * dy), sdir, state_ctx, False)\n        elif cur_t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE:\n            p_pair = self._find_portal_pair(cur_t)\n            if p_pair is None:\n                return\n            px, py = p_pair\n            if self.occupant[py][px] is not None:\n                return\n            if entity == \"BOX\" and (px, py) == (self.hx, self.hy):\n                return\n            state_ctx[\"portal_count\"] += 1\n            self._displace(entity, (x1, y1), (px, py), dname, state_ctx, True)\n\n    def _resolve_explosions(self, initial_bombs, initial_tnts):\n        if not initial_bombs and not initial_tnts:\n            return\n        exploded_bomb_ids = set()\n        exploded_tnts = set()\n        q = collections.deque()\n\n        for b in initial_bombs:\n            if b[\"id\"] not in exploded_bomb_ids:\n                exploded_bomb_ids.add(b[\"id\"])\n                q.append((\"BOMB\", b[\"x\"], b[\"y\"]))\n        for tx, ty in initial_tnts:\n            if (tx, ty) not in exploded_tnts and self.occupant[ty][tx] == \"TNT\":\n                exploded_tnts.add((tx, ty))\n                q.append((\"TNT\", tx, ty))\n\n        bomb_map_by_pos = collections.defaultdict(list)\n        for b in self.bombs:\n            bomb_map_by_pos[(b[\"x\"], b[\"y\"])].append(b)\n\n        sources = []\n        affected_cells = set()\n        while q:\n            stype, ex, ey = q.popleft()\n            sources.append((stype, ex, ey))\n            for ny in range(max(0, ey - 1), min(GRID_HEIGHT, ey + 2)):\n                for nx in range(max(0, ex - 1), min(GRID_WIDTH, ex + 2)):\n                    affected_cells.add((nx, ny))\n                    if self.occupant[ny][nx] == \"TNT\" and (nx, ny) not in exploded_tnts:\n                        exploded_tnts.add((nx, ny))\n                        q.append((\"TNT\", nx, ny))\n                    for b in bomb_map_by_pos.get((nx, ny), ()):\n                        if b[\"id\"] not in exploded_bomb_ids:\n                            exploded_bomb_ids.add(b[\"id\"])\n                            q.append((\"BOMB\", b[\"x\"], b[\"y\"]))\n\n        a = 0\n        b_dmg = 0\n        for stype, ex, ey in sources:\n            if abs(self.hx - ex) <= 1 and abs(self.hy - ey) <= 1:\n                if stype == \"TNT\":\n                    a += 60\n                else:\n                    b_dmg += 50\n\n        if a + b_dmg > 0:\n            eff = min(self.hp, a + b_dmg) * (a / (a + b_dmg))\n            self.trap_damage += eff\n            self.hp -= (a + b_dmg)\n\n        self.bombs = [b for b in self.bombs if b[\"id\"] not in exploded_bomb_ids]\n        for tx, ty in exploded_tnts:\n            if self.occupant[ty][tx] == \"TNT\":\n                self.occupant[ty][tx] = None\n\n        for cx, cy in affected_cells:\n            if self.occupant[cy][cx] == \"BOX\":\n                self.occupant[cy][cx] = None\n            t = self.terrain[cy][cx]\n            if t in EXPLOSION_IMMUNE:\n                pass\n            elif t == \"CRACK\":\n                self.terrain[cy][cx] = \"PIT\"\n            elif t == \"MIMIC\":\n                self._clear_mimic(cx, cy)\n            else:\n                self.terrain[cy][cx] = \"EMPTY\"\n\n        for cy in range(GRID_HEIGHT):\n            for cx in range(GRID_WIDTH):\n                if self.occupant[cy][cx] == \"BOX\" and self.terrain[cy][cx] == \"PIT\":\n                    self.occupant[cy][cx] = None\n                    self.terrain[cy][cx] = \"EMPTY\"\n\n        if self.hp > 0 and self.terrain[self.hy][self.hx] == \"PIT\":\n            dmg = min(self.hp, 100)\n            self.trap_damage += dmg\n            self.hp -= 100\n\n        if self.hp <= 0:\n            self.status = \"DEAD\"\n\n    def compute_firing_lasers_for_turn(self, target_turn: int):\n        \"\"\"Returns list of (lx, ly, dname, period, beam_cells_list, hit_tnt_or_None).\"\"\"\n        fire_red = (target_turn % 3 == 0)\n        fire_blue = (target_turn % 4 == 0)\n        if not fire_red and not fire_blue:\n            return []\n        lasers = []\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT):\n            for x in range(GRID_WIDTH):\n                t = self.terrain[y][x]\n                period = None\n                dname = None\n                if fire_red and t in LASER_RED_DIR:\n                    period = 3\n                    dname = LASER_RED_DIR[t]\n                elif fire_blue and t in LASER_BLUE_DIR:\n                    period = 4\n                    dname = LASER_BLUE_DIR[t]\n                if period is not None:\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n                    beam = []\n                    hit_tnt = None\n                    for dist in range(1, 11):\n                        nx, ny = x + dist * dx, y + dist * dy\n                        if not in_bounds(nx, ny) or self.is_high_wall(nx, ny) or self.occupant[ny][nx] == \"BOX\":\n                            break\n                        if self.occupant[ny][nx] == \"TNT\":\n                            hit_tnt = (nx, ny)\n                            break\n                        beam.append((nx, ny))\n                    lasers.append((x, y, dname, period, beam, hit_tnt))\n        return lasers\n\n    def step(self, action: str):\n        if self.status != \"ALIVE\":\n            return\n        if action not in VALID_ACTIONS:\n            action = \"WAIT\"\n\n        self.turn += 1\n        self.exposed_cells = {(self.hx, self.hy)}\n        tnt_armed = set()\n\n        # Phase A & B\n        if action == \"WAIT\":\n            pass\n        elif action == \"BOMB\":\n            if self.bombs_inv > 0 and not any(b[\"x\"] == self.hx and b[\"y\"] == self.hy for b in self.bombs):\n                self.bombs_inv -= 1\n                self.bombs.append({\"id\": self.next_bomb_id, \"x\": self.hx, \"y\": self.hy, \"timer\": 3})\n                self.next_bomb_id += 1\n        elif action.startswith(\"MOVE_\"):\n            dname = action[5:]\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n            p1x, p1y = self.hx + dx, self.hy + dy\n            if not self.is_high_wall(p1x, p1y) and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, dname) and self.occupant[p1y][p1x] != \"TNT\":\n                if self.occupant[p1y][p1x] == \"BOX\":\n                    p2x, p2y = self.hx + 2 * dx, self.hy + 2 * dy\n                    if self._can_box_enter(p2x, p2y, dname):\n                        box_ctx = {\"steps_taken\": 0, \"portal_count\": 0, \"tnt_armed\": tnt_armed}\n                        self._displace(\"BOX\", (p1x, p1y), (p2x, p2y), dname, box_ctx, False)\n                        if self.status == \"ALIVE\" and self._can_hero_enter(p1x, p1y, dname):\n                            hero_ctx = {\"steps_taken\": 0, \"portal_count\": 0, \"tnt_armed\": tnt_armed}\n                            self._displace(\"HERO\", (self.hx, self.hy), (p1x, p1y), dname, hero_ctx, False)\n                else:\n                    hero_ctx = {\"steps_taken\": 0, \"portal_count\": 0, \"tnt_armed\": tnt_armed}\n                    self._displace(\"HERO\", (self.hx, self.hy), (p1x, p1y), dname, hero_ctx, False)\n        elif action.startswith(\"JUMP_\"):\n            if self.jumps > 0:\n                self.jumps -= 1\n                dname = action[5:]\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n                p1x, p1y = self.hx + dx, self.hy + dy\n                p2x, p2y = self.hx + 2 * dx, self.hy + 2 * dy\n                if (\n                    not self.is_high_wall(p1x, p1y)\n                    and not self.blocks_oneway(p1x, p1y, dname)\n                    and in_bounds(p2x, p2y)\n                    and self._can_hero_enter(p2x, p2y, dname)\n                ):\n                    hero_ctx = {\"steps_taken\": 0, \"portal_count\": 0, \"tnt_armed\": tnt_armed}\n                    self._displace(\"HERO\", (self.hx, self.hy), (p2x, p2y), dname, hero_ctx, False)\n        elif action.startswith(\"THROW_\"):\n            if self.throws > 0:\n                self.throws -= 1\n                dname = action[6:]\n                sx, sy = self.hx, self.hy\n                range_left = 6\n                stone_portals = 0\n                shattered = False\n                while range_left > 0:\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n                    sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                    range_left -= 1\n                    if not in_bounds(sx, sy) or self.is_high_wall(sx, sy) or self.occupant[sy][sx] == \"BOX\":\n                        shattered = True\n                        break\n                    if self.occupant[sy][sx] == \"TNT\":\n                        tnt_armed.add((sx, sy))\n                        shattered = True\n                        break\n                    t = self.terrain[sy][sx]\n                    if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                        self.gates_flipped = not self.gates_flipped\n                        shattered = True\n                        break\n                    if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                        self._clear_mimic(sx, sy)\n                        shattered = True\n                        break\n                    if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        dname = MIRROR_MAP[t][dname]\n                    elif t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and stone_portals < 2:\n                        p_pair = self._find_portal_pair(t)\n                        if p_pair is not None and self.occupant[p_pair[1]][p_pair[0]] not in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                            sx, sy = p_pair\n                            stone_portals += 1\n                            if stone_portals == 2:\n                                shattered = True\n                                break\n                if not shattered and range_left == 0 and self.terrain[sy][sx] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys()):\n                    for _ in range(32):\n                        dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n                        sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                        if not in_bounds(sx, sy) or self.is_high_wall(sx, sy) or self.occupant[sy][sx] == \"BOX\":\n                            break\n                        if self.occupant[sy][sx] == \"TNT\":\n                            tnt_armed.add((sx, sy))\n                            break\n                        t = self.terrain[sy][sx]\n                        if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                            self.gates_flipped = not self.gates_flipped\n                            break\n                        if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                            self._clear_mimic(sx, sy)\n                            break\n                        if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                            dname = MIRROR_MAP[t][dname]\n                        elif t == \"ICE\":\n                            continue\n                        elif t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and stone_portals < 2:\n                            p_pair = self._find_portal_pair(t)\n                            if p_pair is not None and self.occupant[p_pair[1]][p_pair[0]] not in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                                sx, sy = p_pair\n                                stone_portals += 1\n                                if stone_portals == 2:\n                                    break\n                            else:\n                                break\n                        else:\n                            # ONEWAY_* and all other terrains immediately stop ground slide!\n                            break\n\n        if self.status in (\"DEAD\", \"CLEARED\"):\n            return\n\n        # Phase C\n        for b in self.bombs:\n            b[\"timer\"] -= 1\n        due_bombs = [b for b in self.bombs if b[\"timer\"] == 0]\n        if due_bombs or tnt_armed:\n            self._resolve_explosions(due_bombs, tnt_armed)\n            if self.status == \"DEAD\":\n                return\n\n        # Phase D: Dual-frequency lasers (turn % 3 == 0 or turn % 4 == 0)\n        if self.turn % 3 == 0 or self.turn % 4 == 0:\n            lasers = self.compute_firing_lasers_for_turn(self.turn)\n            m = 0\n            laser_tnt_armed = set()\n            for _, _, _, _, beam, hit_tnt in lasers:\n                if hit_tnt is not None:\n                    laser_tnt_armed.add(hit_tnt)\n                if any(cell in self.exposed_cells for cell in beam):\n                    m += 1\n            if m > 0:\n                dmg = min(self.hp, 50 * m)\n                self.trap_damage += dmg\n                self.hp -= 50 * m\n                if self.hp <= 0:\n                    self.status = \"DEAD\"\n                    return\n            if laser_tnt_armed:\n                self._resolve_explosions([], laser_tnt_armed)\n                if self.status == \"DEAD\":\n                    return\n\n        if self.turn >= MAX_TURNS and self.status == \"ALIVE\":\n            self.status = \"TIMEOUT\"\n\n    def get_observation(self) -> dict:\n        next_turn = self.turn + 1\n        vis = [[True] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n        obs_terrain = [\n            [\"CHEST\" if t in (\"HEAL\", \"MIMIC\") else t for t in row]\n            for row in self.terrain\n        ]\n        obs_occ = [row[:] for row in self.occupant]\n        obs_clues = [row[:] for row in self.clues]\n        obs_bombs = [{\"x\": b[\"x\"], \"y\": b[\"y\"], \"timer\": b[\"timer\"]} for b in self.bombs]\n\n        warnings = []\n        if next_turn % 3 == 0 or next_turn % 4 == 0:\n            for lx, ly, dname, period, beam, _ in self.compute_firing_lasers_for_turn(next_turn):\n                for bx, by in beam:\n                    warnings.append({\n                        \"x\": bx, \"y\": by, \"dir\": dname,\n                        \"period\": period, \"cannon\": [lx, ly],\n                    })\n            warnings.sort(key=lambda w: (w[\"y\"], w[\"x\"], w[\"dir\"], w[\"period\"], w[\"cannon\"]))\n\n        return {\n            \"turn\": next_turn,\n            \"turns_left\": MAX_TURNS - self.turn,\n            \"mode\": \"FULL\",\n            \"hero\": {\n                \"x\": self.hx,\n                \"y\": self.hy,\n                \"hp\": self.hp,\n                \"keys\": [self.has_k1, self.has_k2],\n                \"jumps\": self.jumps,\n                \"throws\": self.throws,\n                \"bombs\": self.bombs_inv,\n            },\n            \"gates_flipped\": self.gates_flipped,\n            \"plates_active\": self.is_plates_active(),\n            \"targets\": copy.deepcopy(self.targets),\n            \"visible_mask\": vis,\n            \"terrain\": obs_terrain,\n            \"occupant\": obs_occ,\n            \"bombs\": obs_bombs,\n            \"clues\": obs_clues,\n            \"laser_warnings\": warnings,\n        }\n\n    def compute_scores(self):\n        g = (MAX_BUDGET - self.spent_gold) / MAX_BUDGET\n        D = min(100.0, self.trap_damage)\n        k = int(self.has_k1) + int(self.has_k2)\n        r = max(0.0, (self.stage_d0 - self.stage_dmin) / max(1, self.stage_d0))\n        P = min(2.999999, k + r)\n        if self.status == \"CLEARED\":\n            hero_score = 600.0 + 1.5 * self.hp + 150.0 * (240 - self.turn) / 239.0 + 100.0 * g\n            demon_score = min(320.0, 1.7 * D + 150.0 * (self.turn - 1) / 239.0)\n        elif self.status == \"DEAD\":\n            hero_score = 200.0 * P\n            demon_score = 500.0 + 50.0 * (3.0 - P) + D + 250.0 * g\n        else:\n            hero_score = 200.0 * P\n            demon_score = 320.0 + 50.0 * (3.0 - P) + D + 125.0 * g\n        return hero_score, demon_score\n\n\ndef verify_spawn_4_turn_fairness(decoded: dict):\n    \"\"\"\n    v2.2 Spawn 4-Turn Fairness Rule:\n    At least 2 distinct Turn-1 end coordinates (hx, hy) with 0 trap damage alive/cleared,\n    each having at least one Turn 2->3->4 action sequence with 0 trap damage surviving Turn 4.\n    \"\"\"\n eng.has_k1, eng.has_k2 = h[\"keys\"][0], h[\"keys\"][1]\n    eng.jumps = h[\"jumps\"]\n    eng.throws = h[\"throws\"]\n    eng.bombs_inv = h[\"bombs\"]\n    eng.gates_flipped = state[\"gates_flipped\"]\n    eng.bombs = [\n        {\"id\": idx + 1, \"x\": b[\"x\"], \"y\": b[\"y\"], \"timer\": b[\"timer\"]}\n        for idx, b in enumerate(state[\"bombs\"])\n    ]\n    eng.next_bomb_id = len(eng.bombs) + 1\n    return eng\n\n\ndef compute_laser_info(eng: DungeonEngineV22):\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns (red_cannons_at, blue_cannons_at, period_lcm, near_laser_grid).\n    red_cannons_at[y][x] = bitmask of red cannon IDs hitting (x, y) (exact multi-cannon union!)\n    blue_cannons_at[y][x] = bitmask of blue cannon IDs hitting (x, y)\n    \"\"\"\n    red_mask = [[0] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n    blue_mask = [[0] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n    has_red = False\n    has_blue = False\n    cid = 0\n\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        trow = eng.terrain[y]\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            t = trow[x]\n            if t in LASER_RED_DIR:\n                has_red = True\n                g_out = red_mask\n                dname = LASER_RED_DIR[t]\n            elif t in LASER_BLUE_DIR:\n                has_blue = True\n                g_out = blue_mask\n                dname = LASER_BLUE_DIR[t]\n            else:\n                continue\n            bit = 1 << cid\n            cid += 1\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n            for dist in range(1, 11):\n                nx, ny = x + dist * dx, y + dist * dy\n                if not in_bounds(nx, ny) or eng.is_high_wall(nx, ny) or eng.occupant[ny][nx] in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                    break\n                g_out[ny][nx] |= bit\n\n    if has_red and has_blue:\n        period = 12\n    elif has_blue:\n        period = 4\n    elif has_red:\n        period = 3\n    else:\n        period = 1\n\n    near_laser = [[False] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n    if period > 1:\n        # Mark any cell on a beam or within 2 steps of a beam (or adjacent to ICE/SPRING/PORTAL) as temporal-sensitive!\n        beam_cells = [\n            (x, y)\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1)\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1)\n            if red_mask[y][x] or blue_mask[y][x]\n        ]\n        for bx, by in beam_cells:\n            for dy in range(-2, 3):\n                ny = by + dy\n                if 0 <= ny < GRID_HEIGHT:\n                    for dx in range(-2, 3):\n                        nx = bx + dx\n                        if 0 <= nx < GRID_WIDTH:\n                            near_laser[ny][nx] = True\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                if eng.terrain[y][x] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys(), *SPRING_DIR.keys(), *PORTAL_PAIR_TILE.keys()):\n                    for dx, dy in DIRECTIONS.values():\n                        if in_bounds(x + dx, y + dy):\n                            near_laser[y + dy][x + dx] = True\n                    near_laser[y][x] = True\n\n    return red_mask, blue_mask, period, near_laser\n\n\ndef fast_hero_nav(eng: DungeonEngineV22, allow_damage: bool = False):\n    red_mask, blue_mask, period, near_laser = compute_laser_info(eng)\n    plates_on = eng.is_plates_active()\n    gf = eng.gates_flipped\n    terrain = eng.terrain\n    occupant = eng.occupant\n    portals = eng.portals\n    has_both_keys = eng.has_k1 and eng.has_k2\n    active_bombs = eng.bombs\n    has_bombs = bool(active_bombs)\n\n    def is_hw(x, y):\n        if not in_bounds(x, y):\n            return True\n        t = terrain[y][x]\n        if t in (\"W\", \"T_W\") or t in ALL_LASER_DIR:\n            return True\n        if t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"P_GATE\" and not plates_on:\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    def blk_ow(x, y, dname):\n        t = terrain[y][x]\n        return (t in ONEWAY_DIR) and (ONEWAY_DIR[t] != dname)\n\n    def can_h(x, y, dname):\n        if is_hw(x, y) or blk_ow(x, y, dname):\n            return False\n        if occupant[y][x] in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    def make_skey(x, y, t):\n        if has_bombs or near_laser[y][x]:\n            return (x, y, t % period if period > 1 else (t - eng.turn))\n        return (x, y, -1)\n\n    start_skey = make_skey(eng.hx, eng.hy, eng.turn)\n    best_cost = {start_skey: 0.0}\n    parents = [None]  # node_id -> (prev_id, act)\n    node_meta = [(0.0, eng.turn, eng.hx, eng.hy, eng.hp, eng.jumps)]  # id 0\n    best_at_cell = {(eng.hx, eng.hy): (0.0, 0)}  # (x, y) -> (cost, node_id)\n\n    pq = [(0.0, 0, frozenset())]\n    special_hits = {}  # (kind, tx, ty) -> (cost, prev_node_id, last_act)\n\n    def reconstruct(nid, last_act=None):\n        acts = []\n        if last_act is not None:\n            acts.append(last_act)\n        while parents[nid] is not None:\n            nid, a = parents[nid]\n            acts.append(a)\n        acts.reverse()\n        return tuple(acts)\n\n    while pq:\n        cost, nid, cracks = heapq.heappop(pq)\n        _, cur_t, x, y, hp, jleft = node_meta[nid]\n        skey = make_skey(x, y, cur_t)\n        if cost > best_cost.get(skey, 1e18) + 1e-6:\n            continue\n\n        nturn = cur_t + 1\n        if nturn > MAX_TURNS:\n            continue\n\n        allow_wait = has_bombs or near_laser[y][x]\n        base_acts = (\"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_L\", \"MOVE_R\", \"WAIT\") if allow_wait else (\"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_L\", \"MOVE_R\")\n        if jleft > 0:\n            base_acts = base_acts + (\"JUMP_U\", \"JUMP_D\", \"JUMP_L\", \"JUMP_R\")\n\n        for act in base_acts:\n            nj = jleft\n            nhp = hp\n            step_c = 1.15 if act == \"WAIT\" else 1.0\n\n            if act == \"WAIT\":\n                nx, ny = x, y\n                exp_r = red_mask[y][x]\n                exp_b = blue_mask[y][x]\n                ncracks = cracks\n                special_event = None\n            else:\n                is_j = act[0] == \"J\"\n                dname = act[5:]\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n                if is_j:\n                    p1x, p1y = x + dx, y + dy\n                    tx, ty = x + 2 * dx, y + 2 * dy\n                    if not in_bounds(tx, ty) or is_hw(p1x, p1y) or blk_ow(p1x, p1y, dname):\n                        continue\n                    # Prune useless jump over completely plain safe EMPTY ground\n                    t_mid = terrain[p1y][p1x]\n                    if (\n                        t_mid == \"EMPTY\"\n                        and occupant[p1y][p1x] is None\n                        and (p1x, p1y) not in cracks\n                        and red_mask[p1y][p1x] == 0\n                        and blue_mask[p1y][p1x] == 0\n                    ):\n                        continue\n                    if not can_h(tx, ty, dname) or (tx, ty) in cracks:\n                        continue\n                    nj -= 1\n                    step_c += 18.0\n                else:\n                    tx, ty = x + dx, y + dy\n                    if not can_h(tx, ty, dname) or (tx, ty) in cracks:\n                        continue\n\n                cx0, cy0 = x, y\n                cx1, cy1 = tx, ty\n                cd = dname\n                exp_r = red_mask[y][x]\n                exp_b = blue_mask[y][x]\n                ncr_mut = None\n                p_cnt = 0\n                sup_p = False\n                dead = False\n                instant_win = False\n                special_event = None\n\n                for step_i in range(32):\n                    if terrain[cy0][cx0] == \"CRACK\":\n             step_c += 5.0\n                    if terrain[cy1][cx1] in ONEWAY_DIR:\n                        step_c += 3.5\n\n                    exp_r |= red_mask[cy1][cx1]\n                    exp_b |= blue_mask[cy1][cx1]\n\n                    active_cr = ncr_mut if ncr_mut is not None else cracks\n                    ct = \"PIT\" if ((cx1, cy1) in active_cr and (cx1, cy1) != (cx0, cy0) and terrain[cy1][cx1] == \"CRACK\") else terrain[cy1][cx1]\n                    stop_m = False\n                    if ct == \"PIT\":\n                        dead = True\n                        break\n                    elif ct == \"MIMIC\":\n                        if not allow_damage or nhp <= 45:\n                            dead = True\n                            break\n                        nhp -= 45\n                        step_c += 120.0\n                        stop_m = True\n                        special_event = (\"MIMIC\", cx1, cy1)\n                    elif ct == \"HEAL\":\n   special_event = (\"HEAL\", cx1, cy1)\n                    elif ct == \"SWITCH\":\n                        special_event = (\"SWITCH\", cx1, cy1)\n                    elif ct == \"K1\" and not eng.has_k1:\n                        special_event = (\"K1\", cx1, cy1)\n                    elif ct == \"K2\" and not eng.has_k2:\n                        special_event = (\"K2\", cx1, cy1)\n                    elif ct == \"V\" and has_both_keys and nhp > 0:\n                        instant_win = True\n                        break\n\n                    if step_i == 31 or stop_m or special_event is not None:\n                        break\n\n                    if sup_p:\n                        sup_p = False\n                        if p_cnt >= 2:\n                            break\n                        ddx, ddy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                        nnx, nny = cx1 + ddx, cy1 + ddy\n                        if not can_h(nnx, nny, cd):\n                            break\n                        if (nnx, nny) in active_cr:\n                            dead = True\n                            break\n                        cx0, cy0, cx1, cy1 = cx1, cy1, nnx, nny\n MIRROR_MAP[ct][cd]\n                        ddx, ddy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                        nnx, nny = cx1 + ddx, cy1 + ddy\n                        if not can_h(nnx, nny, cd):\n                            break\n                        cx0, cy0, cx1, cy1 = cx1, cy1, nnx, nny\n                    elif ct in SPRING_DIR:\n                        cd = SPRING_DIR[ct]\n                        ddx, ddy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                        blk_k = None\n                        for kk in (1, 2, 3):\n                            kx, ky = cx1 + kk * ddx, cy1 + kk * ddy\n cx1, cy1, p_pair[0], p_pair[1]\n                    else:\n                        break\n\n                if dead:\n                    continue\n                if instant_win:\n                    return best_at_cell, reconstruct, special_hits, reconstruct(nid, act)\n\n                nx, ny = cx1, cy1\n                ncracks = frozenset(ncr_mut) if ncr_mut is not None else cracks\n\n            if has_bombs:\n                dt_b = nturn - eng.turn\n                if any(b[\"timer\"] == dt_b and abs(nx - b[\"x\"]) <= 1 and abs(ny - b[\"y\"]) <= 1 for b in active_bombs):\n                    continue\n\n            ldmg = 0\n            if nturn % 3 == 0 and exp_r:\n                ldmg += 50 * exp_r.bit_count()\n            if nturn % 4 == 0 and exp_b:\n                ldmg += 50 * exp_b.bit_count()\n\n            if ldmg > 0:\n                if not allow_damage or nhp <= ldmg:\n                    continue\n                nhp -= ldmg\n                step_c += 2.5 * ldmg\n\n            ncost = cost + step_c\n\n            if special_event is not None:\n                if ncost < special_hits.get(special_event, (1e18, None, None))[0]:\n                    special_hits[special_event] = (ncost, nid, act)\n                continue\n\n            nskey = make_skey(nx, ny, nturn)\n            if ncost < best_cost.get(nskey, 1e18):\n                best_cost[nskey] = ncost\n                new_id = len(parents)\n                parents.append((nid, act))\n                node_meta.append((ncost, nturn, nx, ny, nhp, nj))\n                if (nx, ny) not in best_at_cell or ncost < best_at_cell[(nx, ny)][0]:\n                    best_at_cell[(nx, ny)] = (ncost, new_id)\n                heapq.heappush(pq, (ncost, new_id, ncracks))\n\n    return best_at_cell, reconstruct, special_hits, None\n\n\ndef trace_stone_from(eng: DungeonEngineV22, vx: int, vy: int, dname: str):\n    sx, sy = vx, vy\n    rleft = 6\n    sp = 0\n    while rleft > 0:\n        dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n        sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n        rleft -= 1\n        if not in_bounds(sx, sy) or eng.is_high_wall(sx, sy) or eng.occupant[sy][sx] == \"BOX\":\n            return None\n        if eng.occupant[sy][sx] == \"TNT\":\n            return (\"TNT\", sx, sy)\n        t = eng.terrain[sy][sx]\n        if t == \"SWITCH\":\n            return (\"SWITCH\", sx, sy)\n        if t == \"MIMIC\":\n            return (\"MIMIC\", sx, sy)\n        if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n            dname = MIRROR_MAP[t][dname]\n        elif t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and sp < 2:\n            p_pair = eng.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[t])\n            if p_pair is not None and eng.occupant[p_pair[1]][p_pair[0]] not in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                sx, sy = p_pair\n                sp += 1\n                if sp == 2:\n                    return None\n    if eng.terrain[sy][sx] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys()):\n        for _ in range(32):\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n            sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n            if not in_bounds(sx, sy) or eng.is_high_wall(sx, sy) or eng.occupant[sy][sx] == \"BOX\":\n                return None\n            if eng.occupant[sy][sx] == \"TNT\":\n                return (\"TNT\", sx, sy)\n            t = eng.terrain[sy][sx]\n            if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                return (\"SWITCH\", sx, sy)\n            if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                return (\"MIMIC\", sx, sy)\n            if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                dname = MIRROR_MAP[t][dname]\n            elif t == \"ICE\":\n                continue\n            elif t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and sp < 2:\n                p_pair = eng.portals.get(PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[t])\n                if p_pair is not None and eng.occupant[p_pair[1]][p_pair[0]] not in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                    sx, sy = p_pair\n                    sp += 1\n                    if sp == 2:\n                        return None\n                else:\n                    return None\n            else:\n                return None\n    return None\n\n\ndef world_signature(eng: DungeonEngineV22, period: int):\n    boxes = []\n    tnts = []\n    cracks_cnt = 0\n    walls_hash = 0\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        trow = eng.terrain[y]\n        orow = eng.occupant[y]\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            o = orow[x]\n            if o == \"BOX\":\n                boxes.append((x, y))\n            elif o == \"TNT\":\n                tnts.append((x, y))\n            t = trow[x]\n            if t == \"CRACK\":\n                cracks_cnt += 1\n            elif t in (\"W\", *ALL_LASER_DIR.keys(), \"MIMIC\", \"HEAL\", \"PIT\"):\n                walls_hash ^= hash((x, y, t))\n    return (\n        eng.hx, eng.hy, (eng.turn % period) if period > 1 else 0,\n        eng.has_k1, eng.has_k2, eng.gates_flipped,\n        eng.jumps, eng.throws, eng.bombs_inv,\n        tuple(boxes), tuple(tnts), cracks_cnt, walls_hash,\n    )\n\n\ndef is_box_static_corner_deadlock(eng: DungeonEngineV22, bx: int, by: int) -> bool:\n    if eng.terrain[by][bx] == \"PLATE\":\n        return False\n    for dname, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n        for dist in range(1, 11):\n            nx, ny = bx - dist * dx, by - dist * dy\n            if not in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                break\n            t = eng.terrain[ny][nx]\n            if t in ALL_LASER_DIR and ALL_LASER_DIR[t] == dname:\n                return False\n            if t == \"T_W\":\n                break\n\n    def perm_blocked(tx, ty, push_dir, stand_x, stand_y):\n        if not in_bounds(tx, ty) or not in_bounds(stand_x, stand_y):\n            return True\n        if eng.terrain[ty][tx] in (\"T_W\", \"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\") or eng.blocks_oneway(tx, ty, push_dir):\n            return True\n        if eng.terrain[stand_y][stand_x] == \"T_W\" or eng.blocks_oneway(bx, by, push_dir):\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    h_blk = perm_blocked(bx + 1, by, \"R\", bx - 1, by) and perm_blocked(bx - 1, by, \"L\", bx + 1, by)\n    v_blk = perm_blocked(bx, by + 1, \"D\", bx, by - 1) and perm_blocked(bx, by - 1, \"U\", bx, by + 1)\n    return h_blk and v_blk\n\n\ndef heuristic_v22(eng: DungeonEngineV22, has_pgates: bool, goal_pits: list) -> float:\n    h = 0.0\n    k1 = tuple(eng.targets[\"K1\"])\n    k2 = tuple(eng.targets[\"K2\"])\n    v = tuple(eng.targets[\"V\"])\n    pos = (eng.hx, eng.hy)\n\n    if not eng.has_k1 and not eng.has_k2:\n        d_keys = min(\n            manhattan(pos, k1) + manhattan(k1, k2) + manhattan(k2, v),\n            manhattan(pos, k2) + manhattan(k2, k1) + manhattan(k1, v),\n        )\n        h += 75.0 * 2 + 0.9 * d_keys\n    elif not eng.has_k1:\n        h += 75.0 + 0.9 * (manhattan(pos, k1) + manhattan(k1, v))\n    elif not eng.has_k2:\n        h += 75.0 + 0.9 * (manhattan(pos, k2) + manhattan(k2, v))\n    else:\n        h += 0.9 * manhattan(pos, v)\n\n    boxes = [\n        (x, y)\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1)\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1)\n        if eng.occupant[y][x] == \"BOX\"\n    ]\n\n    if has_pgates and eng.plates:\n        uncovered = [p for p in eng.plates if eng.occupant[p[1]][p[0]] != \"BOX\"]\n        free_boxes = [b for b in boxes if eng.terrain[b[1]][b[0]] != \"PLATE\"]\n        h += 45.0 * len(uncovered)\n        if uncovered and free_boxes:\n            for up in uncovered:\n                h += 2.5 * min(manhattan(b, up) for b in free_boxes)\n\n    if goal_pits and boxes:\n        free_boxes = [b for b in boxes if eng.terrain[b[1]][b[0]] != \"PLATE\"]\n        for gp in goal_pits:\n            if eng.terrain[gp[1]][gp[0]] == \"PIT\" and free_boxes:\n                h += 1.8 * min(manhattan(b, gp) for b in free_boxes)\n\n    h += 6.0 * (3 - eng.jumps) + 3.0 * (3 - eng.throws) + 8.0 * (2 - eng.bombs_inv)\n    h += 2.5 * (100 - eng.hp)\n    return h\n\n\ndef solve_full_dungeon(initial_eng: DungeonEngineV22, time_budget: float = 1.55):\n    t0 = time.monotonic()\n    deadline = t0 + time_budget\n\n    has_pgates = any(\n        initial_eng.terrain[y][x] == \"P_GATE\"\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT)\n        for x in range(GRID_WIDTH)\n    )\n    num_plates = len(initial_eng.plates)\n    _, _, init_period, _ = compute_laser_info(initial_eng)\n\n    goal_pits = []\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            if initial_eng.terrain[y][x] == \"PIT\":\n                walls_adj = sum(\n                    1 for dx, dy in DIRECTIONS.values()\n                    if initial_eng.terrain[y + dy][x + dx] in (\"T_W\", \"W\")\n                )\n                if walls_adj >= 2:\n                    goal_pits.append((x, y))\n\n    for allow_dmg in (False, True):\n        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:\n            break\n        sub_deadline = deadline if allow_dmg else (t0 + time_budget * 0.78)\n\n        uid = 0\n        h0 = heuristic_v22(initial_eng, has_pgates, goal_pits)\n        pq = [(h0, 0.0, uid, initial_eng, ())]\n        best_g = {world_signature(initial_eng, init_period): 0.0}\n\n        while pq and time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:\n            f_val, g_val, _, eng, macro_path = heapq.heappop(pq)\n            sig = world_signature(eng, init_period)\n            if g_val > best_g.get(sig, 1e18) + 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            best_at_cell, reconstruct, special_hits, win_nav = fast_hero_nav(eng, allow_damage=allow_dmg)\n            if win_nav is not None:\n                full_sol = list(macro_path + win_nav)\n                v_eng = initial_eng.clone()\n                for a in full_sol:\n                    v_eng.step(a)\n                if v_eng.status == \"CLEARED\":\n                    return full_sol\n\n            def push_child(nav_acts, extra_acts, extra_penalty=0.0):\n                nonlocal uid\n                tot_acts = macro_path + nav_acts + extra_acts\n                if len(tot_acts) > MAX_TURNS:\n                    return None\n                c_eng = eng.clone()\n                hp_before = c_eng.hp\n                for a in nav_acts + extra_acts:\n                    c_eng.step(a)\n                    if c_eng.status in (\"DEAD\", \"CLEARED\"):\n                        break\n                if c_eng.status == \"DEAD\":\n                    return None\n                if not allow_dmg and c_eng.hp < hp_before:\n                    return None\n                if c_eng.status == ## 2026-11-19T20:25:51.627000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=769 message=msg_1f38e4c84001cSWAnjfz9KwZBt

**Analyzing Output Data**

I've struck gold! Line 4 of the output, specifically `glm after ('K1', 18, 5) -> after2 ('K2', 18, 20) pos (18, 20) hp 100 jumps 1 wn3 True`, is a game changer. The `K2 -> K1` transition shows `jumps` decreasing from 3 to 2, then down to 0, which is exactly what I needed. This is significant.


**Uncovering the Bottleneck**

I've made a crucial breakthrough. The key issue lies in how `solve_full_dungeon` handles bomb variations, specifically with `K1 -> K2`. It prioritizes `K2` due to its slightly smaller f-value, which triggers extensive exploration of bomb subtrees. This results in the algorithm exploring hundreds of random bomb options before revisiting `K1`. Furthermore, I've identified that the optimal solution `sol` requires three jumps to handle the pits, but due to incorrect branching at the start, requires bombs. The algorithm's bomb candidate filtering is clearly a bottleneck.


**Identifying the Bug**

Okay, I've had a massive realization! The `make_skey` function in `fast_hero_nav` completely dropped the `jumps_left` variable from the state key. This is a game-changer because it means different numbers of remaining jumps are being lumped together under the same state. For instance, a long walking detour, which uses no jumps, could be incorrectly pruned because its cost is higher than a shorter jump path, even if the jump path has fewer jumps remaining. And the `+18.0` jump penalty in `glm` is why it overwrites zero jump detour.


**Refining Jump Optimization**

I've made further progress in jump optimization. My current focus is refining how `fast_hero_nav` handles jump penalties. I've increased the `JUMP` conservation penalty to `+80.0`, recognizing the value of jumps as non-renewable resources. I've also incorporated `jleft` into `skey`, resulting in a more precise state key. Now I'm concentrating on Pareto dominance on `(cost, -jumps)` to improve state pruning without accidentally cutting jump-preserving routes.

The current selection is failing to prioritize bombs that remove key obstructions.

I've made progress on optimizing bomb candidate selection. My new filtering approach avoids generating useless bombs in walkable wall mazes like `P1_glm`. This refined logic should eliminate many incorrect candidate selections, particularly on maps like `P1_sol`. I'm optimistic this will lead to a more efficient and effective solution.

**Examining Solution Structure**

I'm now zeroing in on the structure of the `P1_sol` solution. My analysis has taken me to understanding the core steps for finding a solution within `P1_sol`. I'm starting to see that understanding this example can guide my work on this project as a whole.


**Analyzing Bomb Placement**

I'm now zeroing in on the bomb placement strategy for `P1_sol`. It looks like dropping the first bomb at `(3, 12)` is crucial for opening a path. I almost missed that the second bomb, placed at `(6, 10)`, wouldn't clear the thick wall at y=12. After a re-evaluation, I realized that placing Bomb #2 at `(4, 11)` can open up the necessary path at y = 11 and y = 12 simultaneously, which I had missed, a critical error on my part.


**Uncovering Bomb Strategy**

I've just had a major breakthrough concerning the bomb placement in `P1_sol`. It's clear that the selection logic overlooked a crucial diagonal impact. Specifically, the bomb at (4, 11) hits (5, 12) *diagonally*, which was overlooked. My `bomb_cands` list *did* include (4, 11), but `dedup_bombs` eliminated it because a neighboring bomb at (5, 10), blocked it!

are wide open EMPTY (0 lasers),\n    keeping ONLY indestructible T_W, ONEWAY_* vector constraints, PORTAL pairs, and PITs\n    (allowing up to eng.jumps + count(BOX) total PIT/reverse-jump crossings!).\n    If K1 (if missing), K2 (if missing), or V cannot be reached even in this relaxed graph,\n    the state is 100% mathematically deadlocked!\n    \"\"\"\n    num_boxes = sum(\n        1 for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1) for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1)\n        if eng.occupant[y][x] == \"BOX\"\n    )\n    max_pit_budget = eng.jumps + num_boxes\n    terrain = eng.terrain\n    portals = eng.portals\n\n    def min_pits_from(sx, sy, targets_set):\n        best_p = {(sx, sy): 0}\n        dq = collections.deque([(0, sx, sy)])\n        while dq:\n            p_used, x, y = dq.popleft()\n            if p_used > best_p.get((x, y), 999):\n                continue\n            if (x, y) in targets_set:\n                return p_used\n            for dname, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n                nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n                if not in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                    continue\n                t = terrain[ny][nx]\n                if t == \"T_W\" or (t in ONEWAY_DIR and ONEWAY_DIR[t] != dname):\n                    continue\n                cost_p = 1 if t == \"PIT\" else 0\n                np_used = p_used + cost_p\n                if np_used <= max_pit_budget and np_used < best_p.get((nx, ny), 999):\n                    best_p[(nx, ny)] = np_used\n                    if cost_p == 0:\n                        dq.appendleft((np_used, nx, ny))\n                    else:\n                        dq.append((np_used, nx, ny))\n                    if t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE:\n                        pp = req.append(tuple(eng.targets[\"K2\"]))\n    v = tuple(eng.targets[\"V\"])\n\n    if len(req) == 0:\n        return min_pits_from(eng.hx, eng.hy, {v}) <= max_pit_budget\n    elif len(req) == 1:\n max_pit_budget\n    else:\n        # Either K1 -> K2 -> V or K2 -> K1 -> V must fit within max_pit_budget!\n        k1, k2 = req[0], req[1]\n        pa = min_pits_from(eng.hx, eng.hy, {k1}) + min_pits_from(k1[0], k1[1], {k2}) + min_pits_from(k2[0], k2[1], {v})\n        if pa <= max_pit_budget:\n            return True\n        pb = min_pits_from(eng.hx, eng.hy, {k2}) + min_pits_from(k2[0], ## 2026-11-19T20:33:37.310000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=971 message=msg_1f3956792001S5AW1yZaOsB16F

**Analyzing Performance Bottlenecks**

I've zeroed in on the performance impact of `can_ever_win_relaxed`. Its repeated calls within `push_child`, specifically the 3 full 0-1 BFSs per box push, are a significant overhead. This slowdown, exemplified by the impact on `V22_BENCH_STRONG`, is striking, going from 703ms to over 12 seconds. It's a key factor.


**Refining Pruning Strategies**

**Investigating Unexpected Outcome**

I'm puzzled. The `can_ever_win_relaxed(e2)` function returned `True` for an entity (`e2`) with `jumps == 2`. The issue seems to be within `min_pits_from` and the cost calculation (`cost_p = 1 if t == "PIT" else 0`). Specifically, I'm focusing on the behavior when moving onto a specific coordinate (32, 13). Something's not right.


**Tracing the Cause**

I've been tracing the path of the entity `e2`, and I realized a critical detail: the assumption of needing multiple jumps was wrong. The function only needs a single jump! My earlier calculations were off because I was misinterpreting the map data. I had failed to correctly process the `sol` data correctly, and now I see how it could incorrectly lead to the problem! The problem comes from the map `p1[13]` at index 37.


**Clarifying Pit Traversal**

I've pinpointed the root issue: the model incorrectly allows 90-degree turns *after* stepping onto a pit when no boxes exist. The flawed assumption is that a jump can be broken into non-straight moves, costing only a single "pit". The correction is to recognize that stepping on a PIT and turning requires a BOX to be placed on the pit, and jumping over the pit costs 1 JUMP! This misinterpretation made `can_ever_win_relaxed` return an incorrect value. This makes total sense now!

## 2026-11-19T20:45:15.013000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1241 message=msg_1f3a00cf70019R927wQzbJRpcF

**Analyzing Performance Changes**

I've cracked the code on all 16 maps, completing `P1_sol` in a brisk 59 steps! The performance difference in `V22_BENCH_STRONG` is also now clear, with a noticeable slowdown from 703ms to 1234-1563ms. I'm deep-diving into the cause for this, which seems to have made `fast_hero_nav` 2.5x slower.


**Pinpointing the Root Cause**

I've zeroed in on the core issue. Adding `jleft` to `make_skey` quadrupled the state space, as expected! On `V22_BENCH_STRONG`, `fast_hero_nav`'s slowdown is directly linked to this change. My reasoning is solid: with the jump penalty at 75, a jump path can NEVER be cheaper than walking around a wall, even the worst possible detour. Removing `jleft` is the solution.

## 2026-11-19T20:45:58.763000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1267 message=msg_1f3a0b7df001VM7Elv7w7Ap0KS

**Achieving Full Coverage**

I've successfully cleared all 16 benchmark maps, achieving a full sweep. I went through `V22_BENCH_WEAK` and `V22_BENCH_STRONG`, along with all 14 part-1 maps. This includes `ASTRA`, `DEEPSEEK`, `SOL`, `HYDROGEN`, `ARGON`, and `CARBON`. I'm confident in the thoroughness of this process.


**Analyzing Performance Bottlenecks**

I'm now zeroing in on the performance bottlenecks revealed by the `V22_BENCH_STRONG` run, specifically the 1234ms execution time. The focus is on where the time went within the `solve_full_dungeon` function. I've begun to analyze the push reachable boxes section, looking at how the function is dealing with each box on `BENCH_STRONG`. It is evident that to handle complex v2.2 maps, significantly faster box-pushing logic is essential. I'm measuring the steps for Hero to push `BOX #1` one cell from (3,9) to (4,9).


**Optimizing Push Efficiency**

I've pinpointed egregious inefficiencies in box-pushing logic. The `push_child` function is excessively costly: `fast_hero_nav` is scanning the entire map unnecessarily. Stone-throw raycasts are executed far too often, given the sparse nature of targets, and neighbor checks on bomb placements are also inefficient. I'm focusing on significant optimizations: reducing raycast complexity, and reducing macro-step size of box pushes to single cell movements. These two changes together promise significant speedups.


**Addressing Interleaving Explosion**

The interleaving combinatorics have become clearer, and the problem is severe: multiple boxes pushed in different orders create an explosion of redundant states. I'm now understanding that the `world_signature`'s `hero_pos` being the actual Hero's coordinates allows interleaving. I have realized that I must ensure a *canonical* hero position within the `world_signature`, by making it the minimal position reachable without jumping. This addresses thousands of redundant interleaved states. This change alone could revolutionize the search.


**Canonicalizing Hero Position**

I've realized that states where the hero is in the same walkable, connected component are identical. This means that I can replace the hero's position in `world_signature` with `min_walkable_cell(eng)`, its top-leftmost reachable cell via walking. This effectively collapses all *k!* box-push interleavings into a single equivalence class, resulting in 100x state reduction on 4–6 box puzzles! I'm now testing this and also exploring heuristic rewards for box-to-plate/pit movements.




## 2026-11-19T20:45:58.763000+09:00 | 工具决策/执行证据 | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1267 message=msg_1f3a0b7df001VM7Elv7w7Ap0KS

```json
{
  "name": "edit",
  "state": {
    "status": "completed",
    "input": {
      "oldString": "def world_signature(eng: DungeonEngineV22, period: int):\n    boxes = []\n    tnts = []\n    cracks_cnt = 0\n    walls_hash = 0\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        trow = eng.terrain[y]\n        orow = eng.occupant[y]\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            o = orow[x]\n            if o == \"BOX\":\n                boxes.append((x, y))\n            elif o == \"TNT\":\n                tnts.append((x, y))\n            t = trow[x]\n            if t == \"CRACK\":\n                cracks_cnt += 1\n            elif t in (\"W\", *ALL_LASER_DIR.keys(), \"MIMIC\", \"HEAL\", \"PIT\"):\n                walls_hash ^= hash((x, y, t))\n    return (\n        eng.hx, eng.hy, (eng.turn % period) if period > 1 else 0,\n        eng.has_k1, eng.has_k2, eng.gates_flipped,\n        eng.jumps, eng.throws, eng.bombs_inv,\n        tuple(boxes), tuple(tnts), cracks_cnt, walls_hash,\n    )",
      "path": "part2_full_mode/exam_rooms\\P2_04_carbon_opencode\\test_solver_v22.py",
      "newString": "def canonical_hero_anchor(eng: DungeonEngineV22):\n    \"\"\"Canonical representative (min cell) of hero's zero-cost reversible walking component.\"\"\"\n    plates_on = eng.is_plates_active()\n    gf = eng.gates_flipped\n    terrain = eng.terrain\n    occupant = eng.occupant\n    sx, sy = eng.hx, eng.hy\n    best = (sx, sy)\n    vis = {(sx, sy)}\n    q = collections.deque([(sx, sy)])\n    while q:\n        x, y = q.popleft()\n        if (x, y) < best:\n            best = (x, y)\n        for dname, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if (nx, ny) in vis or not in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                continue\n            t = terrain[ny][nx]\n            if t not in (\"EMPTY\", \"PLATE\") and not (t == \"R_GATE\" and gf) and not (t == \"B_GATE\" and not gf) and not (t == \"P_GATE\" and plates_on):\n                continue\n            if occupant[ny][nx] is not None:\n                continue\n            # Also require reverse step to be reversible (already true since t is not ONEWAY/ICE/CRACK/SPRING/PORTAL)\n            vis.add((nx, ny))\n            q.append((nx, ny))\n    return best\n\n\ndef world_signature(eng: DungeonEngineV22, period: int):\n    boxes = []\n    tnts = []\n    cracks_cnt = 0\n    walls_hash = 0\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        trow = eng.terrain[y]\n        orow = eng.occupant[y]\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            o = orow[x]\n            if o == \"BOX\":\n                boxes.append((x, y))\n            elif o == \"TNT\":\n                tnts.append((x, y))\n            t = trow[x]\n            if t == \"CRACK\":\n                cracks_cnt += 1\n            elif t in (\"W\", *ALL_LASER_DIR.keys(), \"MIMIC\", \"HEAL\", \"PIT\"):\n                walls_hash ^= hash((x, y, t))\n    anchor = canonical_hero_anchor(eng)\n    return (\n        anchor[0], anchor[1],\n        eng.has_k1, eng.has_k2, eng.gates_flipped,\n        eng.jumps, eng.throws, eng.bombs_inv,\n        tuple(boxes), tuple(tnts), cracks_cnt, walls_hash,\n    )"

## 2026-11-19T20:47:18.186000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1289 message=msg_1f3a1ee1e001WlMH1z511qS1ey

**Analyzing Performance Metrics**

I'm currently fixated on performance metrics. I see `P1_OFFICIAL_01` running `fast_hero_nav` ridiculously fast at `31 ms` per call with just `3` macros, while `V22_BENCH_WEAK` takes a relatively long `407 ms` with `4` macros. That *one* call seems like the culprit here, and I'm determined to understand why.


**Investigating Slowdowns**

I've zeroed in on the `near_laser` function and the nested loops within `compute_laser_info`, specifically lines 117-125 of `test_solver_v22.py`. My focus is on the `V22_BENCH_WEAK` test case. The laser cycle has a period of 12. For each of the 20 beam cells, there are 25 neighbors. Considering the laser's 12 states per neighbor cell, this accounts for the high overhead per call. The `fast_hero_nav` is iterating across those cells 9 times, and is doing this across every step, which is proving to be slow.


**Optimizing Search Strategies**

I'm now contemplating search strategies for pathfinding. Specifically, I realized that waiting near a laser beam doesn't require checking all neighbors. Checking only cells on the beam or one step orthogonally away is sufficient; a huge reduction in the search space. I also plan to compress the `skey` by packing it into a single integer for hashing. Furthermore, I'll explore why the `V22_BENCH_WEAK` test took extra steps and consider a tighter weight for A* search if time permits.


**Designing Dungeon Logic**

I'm now deep in designing `build_dungeon()`, focusing on the v2.2 Demon Dungeon. I'm prioritizing the hard validation rules, aiming for a budget under 3000 G, ideally targeting 1200-1500 G for bonus retention. I have to ensure the outer frame, Spawn layout, and the 4-turn fairness requirement (at least 2 distinct zero-trap-damage coordinates surviving turn 4) are meticulously met, and must all be 100% validated via the `validate_and_decode_grid_v22` function. I'll be designing inside `design_v22_dungeon.py`. I have about 18 minutes.


**Crafting Game Mechanics**

I'm now implementing anti-solver mechanisms. My focus is on creating a challenging game layout. I'm aiming for a layout around 1380 G, using four synergistic techniques, all in the vein of a deadlocked one-way trap. This includes three plates and four boxes, and is designed to defeat a range of solvers. I'm focusing on the first box, which is near spawn.


**Designing Trap Mechanisms**

I'm now refining the anti-solver trap. The focus is on a layout involving `ONEWAY_R` gates and a strategic `PIT` placement. The goal is to force solvers into a deadlock. Specifically, the solver's heuristic prioritizes `B1(5,13)` near `PLATE #1(7,10)`, leading it to an inescapable `PIT` corridor. This is all due to a "4-wide `PIT` moat + `ONEWAY` bend" combination at the bottom-left `y = 22` section of the design.


**Verifying Deadlock Mechanisms**

I'm now focusing on a specific L-shaped corner trap with a two-tile pit. I'm investigating if a hero can jump this 2-tile pit - nope, a hero dies. I'm checking the interaction of `T_W` walls with bombs - bombs don't destroy `T_W`. I'm determining that the only way to escape this pit is to push a box into it.


**Validating Lock Mechanics**

I'm now tracing the consequences of box placements within this layout.  `B1` is the only box that can ever reach (5, 21), and `B2` is tied to a specific plate. `B1` must move to the pit for the hero to get the key. Meanwhile, `B3` must be pushed left through a gate.  Any solver moving `B1` to a specific plate permanently locks it, making the key unreachable.


**Designing Trap Complexity**

I'm now integrating a complex trap involving a switch and laser execution. Placing a switch within a sealed pocket, accessible only through a very specific throw sequence, will open a gate, influencing box movement and plate activation. Simultaneously, I'm designing a "Venus Flytrap" mechanism. A tempting one-way path leads to a corridor with dual-frequency lasers, offering a high-risk route to the key. This adds another layer of challenge.


**Evaluating Deadlock Solutions**

I'm now deeply involved in analyzing a complex deadlock scenario. I'm focusing on the interaction between `ONEWAY_R` gates, plates, and the laser chamber. The initial assessment is promising.  My internal test solver appears to fail due to a critical oversight in the `can_ever_win_relaxed` function, specifically regarding immobilized boxes on plates. The test solver currently doesn't check if a box, once on a plate, can actually move to a PIT. This seems like the key to validating and testing a solution.


**Refining Validation Checks**

I'm now revising the validation checks. The formula I devised, `spare_boxes_for_pits = max(0, total_surviving_boxes - (num_plates if v_needs_pgate else 0))`, is critical to prevent false positives. My relaxed solver *now* instantly detects unreachable pits at turn 5, specifically pinpointing the false-plate trap. This allows me to prune the solution in only `0.1 ms`. The complexity now lies in crafting more efficient game traps.

**Identifying the Cause**

I've pinpointed the source of the `3435` issue. The culprit seems to be the repeated placement of `"T_W"` elements, specifically in `hline` calls like `hline(2, 24, 35, "T_W")`, and `hline(4, 24, 35, "T_W")`, and also via `vline` and a loop. This accumulation across multiple calls explains the problematic outcome; fixing the repeated calls solves the problem.

**Investigating Wall Collision**

I'm puzzled by the collision logic in `design_v22_dungeon.py`. Specifically, I'm examining how the Hero interacts with `(23, 2)` and `(24, 2)` when `(23, 2)` is `"LASER_R"`. If that cell is a `HIGH_WALL`, how does the Hero ever reach the adjacent `"ONEWAY_R"` cell to the right? It seems impossible given the placement. I need to understand this better.

**Analyzing Box Reachability**

I'm now focusing on calculating box reachability. I'm developing a method to quickly determine the possible destinations for each box, under specific constraints. My aim is to efficiently identify scenarios where a box can never reach essential targets like plates or deep pits. I'm observing and verifying that certain box placements, especially in constrained areas, result in highly limited reachability sets. I'm testing the results to ensure that all crucial targets are accounted for in the evaluation.


**Verifying Box Assignments**

I'm now implementing a validity check for box assignments in `test_solver_v22.py`. I'm focusing on ensuring each sink, like a plate or deep pit, can be assigned a distinct box. I'm leveraging a recursive DFS matching algorithm, which, due to the limited number of sinks and boxes (<= 6), runs in mere microseconds. This allows me to instantly prune invalid configurations, such as the "False-Plate Paradox." I'm also adding a `__main__` block to `design_v22_dungeon.py` to verify this.

## 2026-11-19T21:23:27.943000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1456 message=msg_1f3c309b6001kZBo1tlX5ZHiqZ

**Analyzing Initial Condition**

I've made a crucial discovery! I observed that `can_ever_win_relaxed(e)` incorrectly returned `False` for the initial map `e`. Now, I'm examining two potential reasons for this erroneous behavior, trying to understand this counterintuitive outcome. I need to figure out why this occurred right at the very start of the process.


**Clarifying Directional Constraints**

I've made a breakthrough! My focus shifted to `compute_box_push_reach`. I realized that my previous understanding of one-way cell constraints was incorrect. I previously thought that the `ONEWAY_DIR` check applied when *leaving* these cells, but the rules only consider entry. This is critical. Now, I understand why the `can_ever_win_relaxed` failed for the initial map. The `min_res_from` function failed because it double-counted a box used to enter a one-way cell on the return path!


**Understanding The Logic**

I've finally understood the root cause! The initial `can_win` was `False` due to a misinterpretation of how pits function. When a box fills a pit, the pit becomes permanently empty for both entry and exit. My code, specifically in `min_res_from`, was incorrectly double-counting the box usage across independent BFS searches. I will remove the exit restriction in `compute_box_push_reach`, and check segments individually against box and jump constraints. This will ensure correct accounting of box usage and jump consumption.


**Verifying Implementation Changes**

I'm now implementing the fixes and verifying the results. I'm focusing on the jump constraints, making sure they are correctly handled, specifically `(t1-b1) + (t2-b2) <= eng.jumps`. The permanent "EMPTY" pit state after a box is placed is also being verified, in addition to the bipartite matching of pits, plates, and boxes. Also, I'm working to reduce the gold spent on the dungeon design. I've already replaced some walls and tiles to get around 1550 gold spent. This means getting rid of a TW tile near a K2, and walls and TW tiles near plates, replacing some TW tiles near the vault. Now, I'm ready to apply both fixes for the relaxed win condition and the dungeon design!

{
