  "state": {
    "status": "completed",
    "input": {
      "path": "part2_full_mode/exam_rooms\\P2_04_carbon_opencode\\design_v22_dungeon.py",
      "content": "\"\"\"\nCarbon's v2.2 FULL-Mode Demon Dungeon (Lean ~1620G, g=0.460!):\n1) False-Plate Paradox: PLATE #1(8,10) trapped between ONEWAY_R(7,10) & ONEWAY_L(9,10).\n   West has 2 boxes (B1, B2) and 2 exclusive West box-sinks (PLATE #2 & 2-deep PIT(9,20) for K2),\n   separated from Mid by 0G Sokoban top-wall filter (11,1) + T_W divider!\n   Greedy solvers push B1(6,10) 1 step right into ONEWAY_R(7,10)->PLATE #1(8,10), deadlocking K2!\n2) Remote Stone-Throw Switch: THROW_U through ONEWAY_D(16,4) + PIT(16,3) -> MIRROR_NE(16,2)\n   -> 6th-cell ICE(18..19,2) ground slide -> SWITCH(20,2) -> opens R_GATE(11,10) for B3->PLATE #1!\n3) North-East Venus Flytrap: ONEWAY_R(23,1) + 12-beat Red LASER_R + Blue LASER2_U + ICE->P_GATE!\n4) East Vault Sanctum: ONEWAY_R + 12-beat Red+Blue Crossfire + R_GATE + P_GATE -> V(37,13)!\n\"\"\"\n\nimport collections\nimport json\nimport time\n\nfrom v22_judge import (\n    GRID_WIDTH, GRID_HEIGHT, MAX_BUDGET, MAX_AUTHOR_STEPS, DIRECTIONS,\n    MIRROR_MAP, SPRING_DIR, LASER_RED_DIR, LASER_BLUE_DIR, ALL_LASER_DIR,\n    ONEWAY_DIR, PORTAL_PAIR_TILE, TILE_COSTS,\n    validate_and_decode_grid_v22, DungeonEngineV22,\n)\n\n\ndef create_carbon_v22_grid():\n    g = [\n        [\n            \"T_W\" if (x == 0 or x == GRID_WIDTH - 1 or y == 0 or y == GRID_HEIGHT - 1) else \"EMPTY\"\n            for x in range(GRID_WIDTH)\n        ]\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT)\n    ]\n\n    def hline(y, x1, x2, tile=\"W\"):\n        for x in range(min(x1, x2), max(x1, x2) + 1):\n            g[y][x] = tile\n\n    def vline(x, y1, y2, tile=\"W\"):\n        for y in range(min(y1, y2), max(y1, y2) + 1):\n            g[y][x] = tile\n\n    # 1. West-Mid T_W Divider at x=11 (y=2..19) so NO box from Mid can ever enter West except into PLATE #1(8,10)!\n    # Note: (11, 1) is EMPTY against y=0 outer T_W -> Hero walks freely, boxes CANNOT come off y=1!\n    vline(11, 2, 19, \"T_W\")\n\n    # PLATE #1 Sink Trap at (8, 10):\n    g[9][8] = \"T_W\"\n    g[11][8] = \"T_W\"\n    g[10][7] = \"ONEWAY_R\"\n    g[10][8] = \"PLATE\"\n    g[10][9] = \"ONEWAY_L\"\n    g[10][10] = \"ICE\"\n    g[10][11] = \"R_GATE\"\n\n    # BOX #1 at (6, 10) — 1 step left of ONEWAY_R(7,10) False-Plate Bait!\n    g[10][6] = \"BOX\"\n\n    # PLATE #2 at (8, 16) + BOX #2 at (6, 16)\n    g[15][8] = \"W\"\n    g[17][8] = \"W\"\n    g[16][9] = \"W\"\n    g[16][8] = \"PLATE\"\n    g[16][6] = \"BOX\"\n\n    # SOUTH-WEST K2 SHAFT -> K2=(12, 23) (against FREE y=25 outer T_W!)\n    vline(8, 20, 24, \"T_W\")\n    vline(10, 20, 22, \"T_W\")\n    hline(22, 10, 13, \"T_W\")\n    vline(13, 22, 24, \"T_W\")\n    g[20][9] = \"PIT\"\n    g[21][9] = \"PIT\"\n    g[23][12] = \"K2\"\n\n    # 2. MID CHAMBER: Remote ONEWAY + MIRROR + ICE Stone-Throw Switch + Plate #3\n    hline(1, 15, 20, \"W\")\n    hline(3, 17, 20, \"W\")\n    g[2][15] = \"W\"\n    g[2][16] = \"MIRROR_NE\"\n    g[3][16] = \"PIT\"\n    g[4][16] = \"ONEWAY_D\"\n    g[3][15] = \"W\"\n    g[4][15] = \"W\"\n    g[4][17] = \"W\"\n    g[2][18] = \"ICE\"\n    g[2][19] = \"ICE\"\n    g[2][20] = \"SWITCH\"\n    g[2][21] = \"W\"\n\n    # BOX #3 at (14, 10) (for PLATE #1 via R_GATE(11,10)!)\n    g[10][14] = \"BOX\"\n\n    # PLATE #3 at (20, 14) + BOX #4 at (18, 14)\n    g[14][20] = \"PLATE\"\n    g[14][21] = \"W\"\n    g[14][18] = \"BOX\"\n\n    # 3. NORTH-EAST VENUS FLYTRAP & 12-BEAT DUAL-LASER FORTRESS -> K1=(33, 2)\n    hline(3, 22, 35, \"W\")\n    g[2][21] = \"LASER_R\"       # Red (period=3) sweeping x=22..31, y=2!\n    g[1][22] = \"EMPTY\"\n    g[1][23] = \"ONEWAY_R\"      # Venus Flytrap 1-way lock!\n    g[1][24] = \"EMPTY\"\n    g[1][25] = \"W\"\n    g[1][26] = \"EMPTY\"\n    g[1][27] = \"EMPTY\"\n    for xx in range(28, 36):\n        g[1][xx] = \"W\"\n    g[1][32] = \"T_W\"\n    g[1][33] = \"T_W\"\n    g[1][34] = \"T_W\"\n    g[3][32] = \"T_W\"\n    g[3][33] = \"T_W\"\n    g[3][34] = \"T_W\"\n    g[2][35] = \"T_W\"\n\n    g[12][28] = \"LASER2_U\"     # Blue (period=4) hitting (28,2) for 100-DMG crossfire!\n    g[3][28] = \"PIT\"\n\n    g[2][24] = \"MIRROR_NE\"\n    g[2][25] = \"ICE\"\n    for xx in range(28, 32):\n        g[2][xx] = \"ICE\"\n    g[2][32] = \"P_GATE\"\n    g[2][33] = \"K1\"\n    g[2][34] = \"PORTAL_1A\"\n\n    # PORTAL_1B exit at (30, 13) right in front of Vault Sanctum!\n    g[13][30] = \"PORTAL_1B\"\n\n    # 4. EAST VAULT SANCTUM -> V=(37, 13) (against FREE x=39 outer T_W!)\n    hline(12, 32, 38, \"W\")\n    hline(14, 32, 38, \"W\")\n    g[12][36] = \"T_W\"\n    g[12][37] = \"T_W\"\n    g[14][36] = \"T_W\"\n    g[14][37] = \"T_W\"\n    g[13][38] = \"T_W\"\n\n    g[12][34] = \"PIT\"\n    g[14][34] = \"PIT\"\n    g[7][34] = \"LASER_D\"\n    g[19][34] = \"LASER2_U\"\n\n    g[13][32] = \"ONEWAY_R\"\n    g[13][35] = \"R_GATE\"\n    g[13][36] = \"P_GATE\"\n    g[13][37] = \"V\"\n\n    return g\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    grid = create_carbon_v22_grid()\n    ok, msg, dec = validate_and_decode_grid_v22(grid, check_spawn_4turn=True)\n    print(\"v2.2 Validation (incl. 4-turn spawn check):\", ok, msg)\n    assert ok, msg\n\n    by_t = collections.Counter()\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            t = grid[y][x]\n            if TILE_COSTS[t] > 0:\n                by_t[t] += TILE_COSTS[t]\n    print(\"Cost breakdown:\", dict(by_t))\n    print(\"Spent Gold:\", dec[\"spent_gold\"], \"Vault Gold:\", MAX_BUDGET - dec[\"spent_gold\"],\n          f\"(g = {(MAX_BUDGET - dec['spent_gold'])/MAX_BUDGET:.3f})\")\n\n    from test_solver_v22 import decode_full_observation_to_engine, solve_full_dungeon, can_ever_win_relaxed\n    eng = DungeonEngineV22(dec)\n    print(\"Initial can_ever_win_relaxed:\", can_ever_win_relaxed(eng))\n    bad = eng.clone()\n    bad.hx, bad.hy = 5, 10\n    bad.step(\"MOVE_R\")\n    print(\"False-Plate Trap (B1->7,10) pruned? -> can_ever_win_relaxed:\", can_ever_win_relaxed(bad))\n\n    sim_eng = decode_full_observation_to_engine(eng.get_observation())\n    t0 = time.monotonic()\n    sol = solve_full_dungeon(sim_eng, time_budget=1.65)\n    dt_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0\n    print(f\"Solver result: len={len(sol) if sol else None} in {dt_ms:.1f} ms\")\n    if sol:\n        for i, a in enumerate(sol):\n            eng.step(a)\n            assert eng.status == (\"ALIVE\" if i < len(sol) - 1 else \"CLEARED\")\n        print(f\"Verified CLEAR! Steps={eng.turn} (<= {MAX_AUTHOR_STEPS}), HP={eng.hp}\")\n        print(\"AUTHOR_SOLUTION =\", json.dumps(sol))\n"
    },

        "text": "Wrote file successfully: design_v22_dungeon.py"

## 2026-11-19T21:25:06.917000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1482 message=msg_1f3c48c53001DKXZ6e8tqEG0Ju

**Analyzing the Relaxation**

I've hit a snag. The `can_ever_win_relaxed` function is behaving unexpectedly. Specifically, it's returning `True` for a `bad` state when a one-way `B1` plate is at `(7, 10)`, which seems incorrect. I'm digging into why this relaxation is yielding this problematic result.


**Clarifying the Logic**

I've pinpointed the issue with the `can_ever_win_relaxed` function. It seems that the one-way plate at (7, 10) was incorrectly evaluated, allowing `B1` to move out of the expected constraints. The code allowed a push down from (7,10) to (7,11) because both adjacent cells were initially empty! I've also identified why the solver was slow: `B1` had too much freedom to roam in the West Chamber with so many available tiles.


**Optimizing the Path**

I've had a breakthrough! It appears putting an `ICE` chute from (7, 13) to (7, 19) directly into PIT(7, 20) is the optimal strategy. This setup allows B1 to move into the pit with only two pushes instead of thirteen! My calculations show two optimal push paths from B1 at (6,11). Additionally, reducing the number of "T_W" tiles from 44 to 16 by reducing one of the vertical walls! I believe this is a significant step towards a more efficient solution.

## 2026-11-19T21:35:10.863000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1692 message=msg_1f3cdc37f001Zaxgugr2MJFbKg

**Analyzing Movement Mechanics**

I've hit a snag in the movement logic. If a mirror switch is present at a location, specifically (7,6), and the hero attempts to move there from the right, the game seems to be sliding the hero down ice instead of properly interacting with the switch. I'm going to try setting (7,6) as EMPTY instead of MIRROR_SW and repositioning the wall at (6,6) to see if that resolves the issue.


**Refining Interaction Sequences**

I've been working on a new interaction sequence involving a mirror switch and a spring trap. Specifically, I'm now focused on the optimal placement of a box, a mirror switch, an empty space, and a downward-facing spring. The goal is to create a scenario where the hero can manipulate these elements, and I've identified the key coordinates, and am looking into optimal timing of steps, specifically to work with the mirroring actions. I think I'm close to a working solution.


**Simplifying Box Movement**

I've streamlined the box-pushing sequence further. Now, with a box at (6,6), I see I can use a single downward push to send it into a pit, and another rightward push to position it onto a downward spring. This approach requires only two clean pushes, offering a more elegant solution. The target configuration includes ice, a one-way gate, and a pressure plate, and I'm iterating on positioning those components for optimal effect.

**Analyzing the Core Logic**

I've made a breakthrough. I realized the code's behavior at `3010` is due to `SPRING_D(8, 6)` causing a mid-air flight over `(8, 7)` and `(8, 8)`. The previous assignments of `"PIT"` at `g[7][8]` and `g[8][8]` (related to the `80 G` condition) are irrelevant here, even if those cells are empty. This explains the unexpected result.


**Optimizing for Gold Savings**

I've been examining the excessive gold expenditure. The "ICE" placement on y=7..19 appears wasteful; a single "ICE" at (5, 19) is sufficient for B1. Similarly, the 20 "T_W" placements are unnecessary, and I can dramatically cut the cost. I can eliminate `450 G` by optimizing placement and `B1` at `(5, 17)`. Pushing `R` and using a spring, there are no `PIT`s to be used! This should get the cost down to `2150 G`.

      "content": "\"\"\"\nCarbon's v2.2 FULL-Mode Demon Dungeon (Lean ~2180G, 1-Push Binary Paradox):\n1) Binary Choice Box B1 at (5, 17):\n   - Greedy 1-push R -> SPRING_R(6,17) -> lands on PLATE #1(9,17) [DEADLOCK!]\n   - True 1-push D -> ICE(5,18..19) -> fills PIT(5,20) for K2(12,23)!\n2) Remote Stone-Throw Switch: THROW_U from (16,6) through ONEWAY_D + PIT -> MIRROR_NE -> ICE -> SWITCH!\n3) North-East Venus Flytrap (ONEWAY_R + 12-beat Red/Blue Crossfire + ICE->P_GATE) & East Vault!\n\"\"\"\n\nimport collections\nimport json\nimport time\n\nfrom v22_judge import (\n    GRID_WIDTH, GRID_HEIGHT, MAX_BUDGET, MAX_AUTHOR_STEPS, DIRECTIONS,\n    MIRROR_MAP, SPRING_DIR, LASER_RED_DIR, LASER_BLUE_DIR, ALL_LASER_DIR,\n    ONEWAY_DIR, PORTAL_PAIR_TILE, TILE_COSTS,\n    validate_and_decode_grid_v22, DungeonEngineV22,\n)\n\n\ndef create_carbon_v22_grid():\n    g = [\n        [\n            \"T_W\" if (x == 0 or x == GRID_WIDTH - 1 or y == 0 or y == GRID_HEIGHT - 1) else \"EMPTY\"\n            for x in range(GRID_WIDTH)\n        ]\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT)\n    ]\n\n    def hline(y, x1, x2, tile=\"W\"):\n        for x in range(min(x1, x2), max(x1, x2) + 1):\n            g[y][x] = tile\n\n    def vline(x, y1, y2, tile=\"W\"):\n        for y in range(min(y1, y2), max(y1, y2) + 1):\n            g[y][x] = tile\n\n    # 1. Indestructible West-Mid Divider at x=11 (y=2..21), Hero passes via (11,1)=EMPTY!\n    vline(11, 2, 21, \"T_W\")\n\n    # Binary Choice Box B1 at (5, 17):\n    # - Push R: SPRING_R(6,17) -> flies over (7,17), ONEWAY_R(8,17) -> lands on PLATE #1(9,17)!\n    # - Push D: ICE(5,18..19) -> fills PIT(5,20) for K2(12,23)!\n    g[17][5] = \"BOX\"           # B1!\n    g[17][6] = \"SPRING_R\"\n    g[17][8] = \"ONEWAY_R\"\n    g[16][8] = \"T_W\"\n    g[18][8] = \"T_W\"\n    g[16][9] = \"T_W\"\n    g[18][9] = \"T_W\"\n    g[16][10] = \"T_W\"\n    g[18][10] = \"T_W\"\n    g[17][9] = \"PLATE\"         # PLATE #1!\n    g[17][10] = \"ONEWAY_L\"\n    g[17][11] = \"R_GATE\"\n\n    # Express 2-tile ICE Chute into K2 shaft -> K2=(12, 23)!\n    g[18][5] = \"ICE\"\n    g[19][5] = \"ICE\"\n    vline(4, 20, 24, \"T_W\")\n    vline(6, 20, 22, \"T_W\")\n    hline(22, 6, 13, \"T_W\")\n    vline(13, 22, 24, \"T_W\")\n    g[20][5] = \"PIT\"\n    g[21][5] = \"PIT\"\n    g[23][12] = \"K2\"\n\n    # PLATE #2 at (2, 17) + BOX #2 at (2, 16) (1 push D!)\n    g[16][1] = \"W\"\n    g[16][3] = \"W\"\n    g[17][1] = \"W\"\n    g[17][3] = \"W\"\n    g[18][2] = \"W\"\n    g[16][2] = \"BOX\"           # B2!\n    g[17][2] = \"PLATE\"         # PLATE #2!\n\n    # 2. MID CHAMBER: Remote ONEWAY + MIRROR + ICE Stone-Throw Switch + B3 + B4/Plate #3\n    hline(1, 15, 20, \"W\")\n    hline(3, 17, 20, \"W\")\n    g[2][15] = \"W\"\n    g[2][16] = \"MIRROR_NE\"\n    g[3][16] = \"PIT\"\n    g[4][16] = \"ONEWAY_D\"\n    g[3][15] = \"W\"\n    g[4][15] = \"W\"\n    g[4][17] = \"W\"\n    g[2][18] = \"ICE\"\n    g[2][19] = \"ICE\"\n    g[2][20] = \"SWITCH\"\n    g[2][21] = \"W\"\n\n    # B3 at (12, 17) -> once R_GATE(11, 17) opens, 3 pushes L into PLATE #1(9, 17)!\n    g[16][12] = \"W\"\n    g[18][12] = \"W\"\n    g[17][12] = \"BOX\"          # B3!\n\n    # B4 at (18, 12) -> 1 push R into PLATE #3(19, 12)!\n    g[11][18] = \"W\"\n    g[13][18] = \"W\"\n    g[11][19] = \"W\"\n    g[13][19] = \"W\"\n    g[12][20] = \"W\"\n    g[12][18] = \"BOX\"          # B4!\n    g[12][19] = \"PLATE\"        # PLATE #3!\n\n    # 3. NORTH-EAST VENUS FLYTRAP & 12-BEAT DUAL-LASER FORTRESS -> K1=(33, 2)\n    hline(3, 22, 35, \"W\")\n    g[2][21] = \"LASER_R\"       # Red (period=3) sweeping x=22..31, y=2!\n    g[1][22] = \"EMPTY\"\n    g[1][23] = \"ONEWAY_R\"      # Venus Flytrap 1-way lock!\n    g[1][24] = \"EMPTY\"\n    g[1][25] = \"W\"\n    g[1][26] = \"EMPTY\"\n    g[1][27] = \"EMPTY\"\n    for xx in range(28, 35):\n        g[1][xx] = \"W\"\n    g[1][32] = \"T_W\"\n    g[3][32] = \"T_W\"\n    g[2][35] = \"T_W\"\n    g[1][35] = \"W\"\n\n    g[12][28] = \"LASER2_U\"     # Blue (period=4) hitting (28,2) for 100-DMG crossfire!\n    g[3][28] = \"PIT\"\n\n    g[2][24] = \"MIRROR_NE\"\n    g[2][25] = \"ICE\"\n    for xx in range(28, 32):\n        g[2][xx] = \"ICE\"\n    g[2][32] = \"P_GATE\"\n    g[2][33] = \"K1\"\n    g[2][34] = \"PORTAL_1A\"\n\n    # PORTAL_1B exit at (30, 13) right in front of Vault Sanctum!\n    g[13][30] = \"PORTAL_1B\"\n\n    # 4. EAST VAULT SANCTUM -> V=(37, 13) (against FREE x=39 outer T_W!)\n    hline(12, 32, 38, \"W\")\n    hline(14, 32, 38, \"W\")\n    g[12][36] = \"T_W\"\n    g[12][37] = \"T_W\"\n    g[14][36] = \"T_W\"\n    g[14][37] = \"T_W\"\n    g[13][38] = \"T_W\"\n\n    g[12][34] = \"PIT\"\n    g[14][34] = \"PIT\"\n    g[7][34] = \"LASER_D\"\n    g[19][34] = \"LASER2_U\"\n\n    g[13][32] = \"ONEWAY_R\"\n    g[13][35] = \"R_GATE\"\n    g[13][36] = \"P_GATE\"\n    g[13][37] = \"V\"\n\n    return g\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    grid = create_carbon_v22_grid()\n    ok, msg, dec = validate_and_decode_grid_v22(grid, check_spawn_4turn=True)\n    print(\"v2.2 Validation (incl. 4-turn spawn check):\", ok, msg)\n    assert ok, msg\n\n    by_t = collections.Counter()\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            t = grid[y][x]\n            if TILE_COSTS[t] > 0:\n                by_t[t] += TILE_COSTS[t]\n    print(\"Cost breakdown:\", dict(by_t))\n    print(\"Spent Gold:\", dec[\"spent_gold\"], \"Vault Gold:\", MAX_BUDGET - dec[\"spent_gold\"],\n          f\"(g = {(MAX_BUDGET - dec['spent_gold'])/MAX_BUDGET:.3f})\")\n\n    from test_solver_v22 import decode_full_observation_to_engine, solve_full_dungeon, can_ever_win_relaxed\n    eng = DungeonEngineV22(dec)\n    print(\"Initial can_ever_win_relaxed:\", can_ever_win_relaxed(eng))\n    bad = eng.clone()\n    bad.hx, bad.hy = 4, 17\n    bad.step(\"MOVE_R\")\n    print(\"False-Plate Trap (B1->PLATE #1) box at (9,17)?\", bad.occupant[17][9],\n          \"| pruned? -> can_ever_win_relaxed:\", can_ever_win_relaxed(bad))\n\n    sim_eng = decode_full_observation_to_engine(eng.get_observation())\n    t0 = time.monotonic()\n    sol = solve_full_dungeon(sim_eng, time_budget=1.65)\n    dt_ms = (time.monotonic() - t0) * 1000.0\n    print(f\"Solver result: len={len(sol) if sol else None} in {dt_ms:.1f} ms\")\n    if sol:\n        for i, a in enumerate(sol):\n            eng.step(a)\n            assert eng.status == (\"ALIVE\" if i < len(sol) - 1 else \"CLEARED\")\n        print(f\"Verified CLEAR! ## 2026-11-19T21:38:08.256000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1738 message=msg_1f3d07877001QGHp5LEToG7geW

**Investigating A Critical Anomaly**

I've hit a major snag! I've been reviewing the output and, on pop #3, there's a serious inconsistency. The hero reaches `K1 = (33, 2)` but `pl` is `False`, meaning `P_GATE(32, 2)` should have been closed. This is impossible without some kind of flaw in the simulation! I need to re-examine the logic.


**Analyzing The Unexpected Portal**

I've made a breakthrough! The hero's seemingly impossible backwards teleport is due to portal interactions. It seems they walked into PORTAL_1B, teleported BACKWARDS into PORTAL_1A, and then stepped onto K1! This explains why pop #3's state could not have happened under the previous assumptions. I've also identified the performance bottleneck: can_ever_win_relaxed inside push_child is extremely slow.


**Optimizing the Win Condition**

I've hit a major optimization opportunity! The `compute_box_push_reach` function is completely static, which is fantastic news for caching. I've also figured out I only need to call `can_ever_win_relaxed` under very specific conditions: box movements, disappearances, decreasing jumps, or `ONEWAY` crossings. This should provide major performance gains. Additionally, I've got another crucial fix: I've sealed the backdoor into `PORTAL_1B` by strategically placing `ONEWAY_R` and walls, which will prevent backward portal entries entirely.

## 2026-11-19T21:39:36.179000+09:00 | reasoning | <LOCAL_USER_HOME>\.local\share\opencode\opencode.db session=ses_e1f81f5d2ffeEcNqEZUPLt4y8T seq=1782 message=msg_1f3d1cfe4001sfciG6NcU3snDJ

**Analyzing Rapid Findings**

Wow! I can hardly believe it. My solver, "Res," nailed a solution in a mere 25 pops! That's incredible. Further, our own solver acted as a bug-hunting red-team for the "Demon Dungeon." It uncovered two significant issues during its run. Pops 5, 6, and 7: THROW_U at (16, 5) toggled gf: False -> True -> False. This is exciting progress!


**Refining Bug-Proofing**

I've made some significant progress on bug-proofing. I adjusted the heuristic to penalize throwing more aggressively, fixing an issue where throws were almost free. I also discovered a bypass to a P_GATE, which required a bomb. To address that weakness, I'm making K1 bomb-proof by placing T_W tiles at (33,3) and (34,3). I also adjusted walling to be 3-thick where possible to reduce gold spent.


**Optimizing Chamber Costs**

I've been drilling down on cost optimization. The current design is already cheaper than `BENCH_STRONG`, and all chambers are bomb-proof. Now, I'm focusing on shaving off another 500G without compromising bomb-proof integrity. Specifically, I realized `K2` could be shifted left, which opens up cost-saving opportunities by reducing `"T_W"` tiles. I've re-positioned `PLATE #1` to facilitate the shift to `x = 10`. I have already sealed and tested the cost savings.

This success reveals valuable insights into the design of our dungeon and hints at vulnerabilities we can address.


**Refining Vulnerabilities**

I've made significant progress by focusing on vulnerabilities discovered in the solution. I identified an inefficient sequence of throws that I'm optimizing. Then, the solver employed a Bomb at (33,3) to access a key. I've secured that location and its immediate neighbor with "T_W" tiles to prevent similar tactics. Finally, I've dramatically cut down on Spent Gold, by replacing some "T_W" tiles with walls, resulting in significant savings.


**Optimizing Gold Expenditure**

I'm now diving deep into cost optimization. Replacing the vertical "T_W" tiles at x=11 with a strategic horizontal line from x=2 to 11 at y=15, and adding a short vertical extension, dramatically slashes gold expenditure. This ensures the integrity of the `compute_box_push_reach` function remains intact, while reducing overall cost. There is the option to move the divider from x=11 to surround ONLY West bottom-left, keeping `Spent Gold` at **`~2,450 G <= 3,000 G`** with guaranteed function integrity. I'm double-checking the exact cost savings to confirm these assumptions and calculations.

**Analyzing Solver's Actions**

I'm frustrated. The solver made inefficient choices early on, wasting moves on bombs that ultimately didn't contribute to a solution. Specifically, bombing into cells outside of `no_jump_cells` when `P_GATE` was closed seems like a misstep. I need to understand why these decisions were made.


**Fixing Bomb Penalty**

I realized the solver's inefficient bombing stemmed from a low penalty for unnecessary bombs. The Switch Box, specifically the `"W"` walls around it, highlighted this. I found that the `extra_penalty` for bombs near active targets was too low, especially when compared to pushing boxes. Adjusting this single parameter in `test_solver_v22.py` to `32.0` immediately improves the solver. This ensures pushes are prioritized over wasteful bombing in many cases.

**Analyzing Heuristic Errors**

I can't believe I missed this! The goal pit heuristic was hilariously flawed. Those `goal_pits` I was using included laser window pits! This caused a massive penalty for putting boxes on plates because a box would be removed from `free_boxes`, and the heuristic's minimum manhattan distance suddenly increased, *punishing* progress!! I've removed this from heuristic calculations entirely.

  \"targets\": eng[\"targets\"],\n        \"hx\": eng[\"hx\"],\n        \"hy\": eng[\"hy\"],\n        \"hp\": eng[\"hp\"],\n        \"has_k1\": eng[\"has_k1\"],\n        \"has_k2\": eng[\"has_k2\"],\n        \"jumps\": eng[\"jumps\"],\n        \"throws\": eng[\"throws\"],\n        \"bombs_inv\": eng[\"bombs_inv\"],\n        \"gates_flipped\": eng[\"gates_flipped\"],\n        \"bombs\": [dict(b) for GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n    portals = {}\n    plates = []\n\n    for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT):\n        for x in range(GRID_WIDTH):\n            occupant[y][x] = obs_o[y][x]\n            t = obs_t[y][x]\n            if t == \"CHEST\":\n                is_m = False\n                for dname in (\"U\", \"D\", \"L\", \"R\"):\n                    nx, ny = x + DIRECTIONS[dname][0], y + DIRECTIONS[dname][1]\n                    if _in_bounds(nx, ny) and obs_c[ny][nx]:\n                        is_m = True\n                        break\n                terrain[y][x] = \"MIMIC\" if is_m else \"HEAL\"\n            else:\n                terrain[y][x] = t\n                if t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE:\n                    portals[t] = (x, y)\n                elif t == \"PLATE\":\n eng[\"hy\"] = x1, y1\n        ctx[\"exposed\"].add((x1, y1))\n\n    t1 = terrain[y1][x1]\n    stop_momentum = False\n    if entity == \"BOX\":\n        if t1 == \"PIT\":\n            occupant[y1][x1] eng[\"gates_flipped\"] = not eng[\"gates_flipped\"]\n    else:\n        if t1 == \"PIT\":\n            eng[\"hp\"] -= 100\n        elif t1 == \"MIMIC\":\n            eng[\"hp\"] -= 45\n            terrain[y1][x1] = \"EMPTY\"\n            stop_momentum = True\n        elif t1 == \"HEAL\":\n            eng[\"hp\"] = min(100, eng[\"hp\"] + 30)\n            terrain[y1][x1] = \"EMPTY\"\n        elif eng[\"has_k2\"] = True\n            terrain[y1][x1] = \"EMPTY\"\n        elif ct == \"V\" and eng[\"has_k1\"] and eng[\"has_k2\"] and eng[\"hp\"] > 0:\n            eng[\"status\"] = \"CLEARED\"\n  if suppress_portal_once:\n        if ctx[\"portal_count\"] >= 2:\n            return\n        dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n        nx, ny = x1 + dx, y1 + dy\n        if can_enter(eng, nx, ny, ctx[\"tnt_armed\"].add((kx, ky))\n                break\n        if blocked_k == 1:\n            return\n        tk = (blocked_k - 1) if blocked_k in (2, 3) else 3\n        _eng_displace(eng, entity, return\n        px, py = p_pair\n        if occupant[py][px] is not None:\n            return\n        if entity == \"BOX\" and (px, py) == (eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"]):\n            return\n        ctx[\"portal_count\"] += 1\n        _eng_displace(eng, entity, (x1, y1), (px, py), dname, ctx, True)\n\n\ndef _eng_resolve_explosions(eng: dict, initial_bombs: list, initial_tnts: set) -> None:\n bomb_by_pos = collections.defaultdict(list)\n    for b in eng[\"bombs\"]:\n        bomb_by_pos[(b[\"x\"], b[\"y\"])].append(b)\n\n    sources = []\n    affected = set()\n    while q:\n        st, ex, ey = q.popleft()\n        sources.append((st, ex, ey))\n        for ny in range(max(0, ey - 1), min(GRID_HEIGHT, ey + 2)):\n            for nx in range(max(0, ex - 1), min(GRID_WIDTH, ex + 2)):\n terrain[cy][cx] = \"PIT\"\n        else:\n            terrain[cy][cx] = \"EMPTY\"\n\n    for cy in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        for cx in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            if occupant = eng[\"occupant\"]\n    exposed = {(eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"])}\n    tnt_armed = set()\n\n    if action == \"WAIT\":\n        pass\n    elif action == \"BOMB\":\n        if eng[\"bombs_inv\"] if occupant[p1y][p1x] == \"BOX\":\n                p2x, p2y = eng[\"hx\"] + 2 * dx, eng[\"hy\"] + 2 * dy\n                if _eng_can_box_enter(eng, p2x, p2y, dname):\n                    bctx = {\"steps_taken\": 0, \"portal_count\": 0, \"tnt_armed\": tnt_armed, \"exposed\": exposed}\n                    _eng_displace(eng, \"BOX\", (p1x, p1y), (p2x, p2y), dname, bctx, False)\n action.startswith(\"JUMP_\"):\n        if eng[\"jumps\"] > 0:\n            eng[\"jumps\"] -= 1\n            dname = action[5:]\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n            p1x, p1y = {\"steps_taken\": 0, \"portal_count\": 0, \"tnt_armed\": tnt_armed, \"exposed\": exposed}\n                _eng_displace(eng, \"HERO\", (eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"]), (p2x, p2y), dname, hctx, False)\n    elif action.startswith(\"THROW_\"):\n        if eng[\"throws\"] > 0:\n            eng[\"throws\"] -= 1\n            dname = action[6:]\n            sx, sy = eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"]\n            rleft = 6\n            sp = 0\n            shattered = False\n            while rleft > 0:\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n                sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n                rleft -= 1\n                if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or _eng_is_high_wall(eng, sx, sy) or occupant[sy][sx] == \"BOX\":\n                    shattered = True\n                    break\n                if occupant[sy][sx] == \"TNT\":\n                    tnt_armed.add((sx, sy))\n                    shattered = True\n                    break\n                t = terrain[sy][sx]\n                if t ==   elif t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and sp < 2:\n                    pp = eng[\"portals\"].get(PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[t])\n                    if pp is not None and occupant[pp[1]][pp[0]] not in (\"BOX\",           if not shattered and rleft == 0 and terrain[sy][sx] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys()):\n                for _ in range(32):\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n occupant[sy][sx] == \"TNT\":\n                        tnt_armed.add((sx, sy))\n                        break\n                    t = terrain[sy][sx]\n                    if t == \"SWITCH\":\n           elif t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and sp < 2:\n                        pp = eng[\"portals\"].get(PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[t])\n                        if pp is not None and occupant[pp[1]][pp[0]] not in eng[\"bombs\"]:\n        b[\"timer\"] -= 1\n    due_bombs = [b for b in eng[\"bombs\"] if b[\"timer\"] == 0]\n    if due_bombs or tnt_armed:\n        _eng_resolve_explosions(eng, due_bombs, tnt_armed)\n        if eng[\"status\"] == \"DEAD\":\n            return\n\n    if turn % 3 == 0 or turn % 4 == 0:\n        fire_red = (turn % 3 == 0)\n        fire_blue = (turn % 4 == 0)\n        m = 0\n        laser_tnt_armed = set()\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n            trow = terrain[y]\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                t = trow[x]\n                dname = None\n                if fire_red and t in LASER_RED_DIR:\n                    dname = LASER_RED_DIR[t]\n                elif fire_blue and t in LASER_BLUE_DIR:\n                    dname = LASER_BLUE_DIR[t]\n                if dname is not None:\n                    dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n                    hit_hero = False\n                    for dist in range(1, 11):\n eng[\"hp\"] <= 0:\n                eng[\"status\"] = \"DEAD\"\n                return\n        if laser_tnt_armed:\n            _eng_resolve_explosions(eng, [], laser_tnt_armed)\n            if eng[\"status\"] == \"DEAD\":\n                return\n\n    if turn >= MAX_TURNS and eng[\"status\"] == \"ALIVE\":\n        eng[\"status\"] = \"TIMEOUT\"\n\n\n# ==============================================================================\n\ndef _compute_laser_info(eng: dict):\n    red_mask = [[0] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n    blue_mask = [[0] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n    has_red = False\n    has_blue = False\n    cid = 0\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n    occupant = eng[\"occupant\"]\n\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        trow = terrain[y]\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            t = trow[x]\n            if t in LASER_RED_DIR:\n                has_red = True\n                g_out = red_mask\n                dname = LASER_RED_DIR[t]\n            elif t in LASER_BLUE_DIR:\n                has_blue = True\n                g_out = blue_mask\n                dname = LASER_BLUE_DIR[t]\n            else:\n                continue\n            bit = 1 << cid\n            cid += 1\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n            for dist in range(1, 11):\n                nx, ny = x + dist * dx, y + dist * dy\n                if not _in_bounds(nx, ny) or _eng_is_high_wall(eng, nx, ny) or occupant[ny][nx] in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                    break\n                g_out[ny][nx] |= bit\n\n    period = 12 if (has_red and has_blue) else (4 if has_blue else (3 if has_red else 1))\n    near_laser = [[False] * GRID_WIDTH for _ in range(GRID_HEIGHT)]\n    if period > 1:\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                if red_mask[y][x] or blue_mask[y][x]:\n                    near_laser[y][x] = True\n                    for dx, dy in DIRECTIONS.values():\n                        nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n                        if _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                            near_laser[ny][nx] = True\n        for _ in range(4):\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    if not near_laser[y][x] and terrain[y][x] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys(), *SPRING_DIR.keys(), *PORTAL_PAIR_TILE.keys()):\n                        for dx, dy in DIRECTIONS.values():\n                            if _in_bounds(x + dx, y + dy) and near_laser[y + dy][x + dx]:\n                                near_laser[y][x] = True\n                                break\n        for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                if near_laser[y][x] and terrain[y][x] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys(), *SPRING_DIR.keys(), *PORTAL_PAIR_TILE.keys()):\n                    for dx, dy in DIRECTIONS.values():\n                        if _in_bounds(x + dx, y + dy):\n                            near_laser[y + dy][x + dx] = True\n\n    return red_mask, blue_mask, period, near_laser\n\n\ndef _fast_hero_nav(eng: dict, allow_damage: bool = False):\n    red_mask, blue_mask, period, near_laser = _compute_laser_info(eng)\n    plates_on = _eng_plates_active(eng)\n    gf = eng[\"gates_flipped\"]\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n    occupant = eng[\"occupant\"]\n    portals = eng[\"portals\"]\n    has_both_keys = eng[\"has_k1\"] and eng[\"has_k2\"]\n    active_bombs = eng[\"bombs\"]\n    has_bombs = bool(active_bombs)\n    t0_eng = eng[\"turn\"]\n\n    def is_hw(x, y):\n        if not _in_bounds(x, y):\n            return True\n        t = terrain[y][x]\n        if t in (\"W\", \"T_W\") or t in ALL_LASER_DIR:\n            return True\n        if t == \"R_GATE\" and not gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"B_GATE\" and gf:\n            return True\n        if t == \"P_GATE\" and not plates_on:\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    def blk_ow(x, y, dname):\n        t = terrain[y][x]\n        return (t in ONEWAY_DIR) and (ONEWAY_DIR[t] != dname)\n\n    def can_h(x, y, dname):\n        if is_hw(x, y) or blk_ow(x, y, dname):\n            return False\n        if occupant[y][x] in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n            return False\n        return True\n\n    def make_skey(x, y, t):\n        if has_bombs or near_laser[y][x]:\n            return (x, y, t % period if period > 1 else (t - t0_eng))\n        return (x, y, -1)\n\n    start_skey = make_skey(eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"], t0_eng)\n    best_cost = {start_skey: 0.0}\n    parents = [None]\n    node_meta = [(0.0, t0_eng, eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"], eng[\"hp\"], eng[\"jumps\"])]\n    best_at_cell = {(eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"]): (0.0, 0, eng[\"jumps\"])}\n\n    pq = [(0.0, 0, frozenset())]\n    special_hits = {}\n\n    def reconstruct(nid, last_act=None):\n        acts = []\n        if last_act is not None:\n            acts.append(last_act)\n        while parents[nid] is not None:\n            nid, a = parents[nid]\n            acts.append(a)\n        acts.reverse()\n        return tuple(acts)\n\n    while pq:\n        cost, nid, cracks = heapq.heappop(pq)\n        _, cur_t, x, y, hp, jleft = node_meta[nid]\n        skey = make_skey(x, y, cur_t)\n        if cost > best_cost.get(skey, 1e18) + 1e-6:\n            continue\n\n        nturn = cur_t + 1\n        if nturn > MAX_TURNS:\n            continue\n\n        allow_wait = has_bombs or near_laser[y][x]\n        base_acts = (\"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_L\", \"MOVE_R\", \"WAIT\") if allow_wait else (\"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_L\", \"MOVE_R\")\n        if jleft > 0:\n            base_acts = base_acts + (\"JUMP_U\", \"JUMP_D\", \"JUMP_L\", \"JUMP_R\")\n\n        for act in base_acts:\n            nj = jleft\n            nhp = hp\n            step_c = 1.15 if act == \"WAIT\" else 1.0\n\n            if act == \"WAIT\":\n                nx, ny = x, y\n                exp_r = red_mask[y][x]\n                exp_b = blue_mask[y][x]\n                ncracks = cracks\n                special_event = None\n            else:\n                is_j = act[0] == \"J\"\n                dname = act[5:]\n                dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n                if is_j:\n                    p1x, p1y = x + dx, y + dy\n                    tx, ty = x + 2 * dx, y + 2 * dy\n                    if not _in_bounds(tx, ty) or is_hw(p1x, p1y) or blk_ow(p1x, p1y, dname):\n                        continue\n                    t_mid = terrain[p1y][p1x]\n                    if (\n                        t_mid == \"EMPTY\"\n                        and occupant[p1y][p1x] is None\n                        and (p1x, p1y) not in cracks\n                        and red_mask[p1y][p1x] == 0\n                        and blue_mask[p1y][p1x] == 0\n                    ):\n                        continue\n                    if not can_h(tx, ty, dname) or (tx, ty) in cracks:\n                        continue\n                    nj -= 1\n                    step_c += 75.0\n                else:\n                    tx, ty = x + dx, y + dy\n                    if not can_h(tx, ty, dname) or (tx, ty) in cracks:\n                        continue\n\n                cx0, cy0 = x, y\n                cx1, cy1 = tx, ty\n                cd = dname\n                exp_r = red_mask[y][x]\n                exp_b = blue_mask[y][x]\n                ncr_mut = None\n                p_cnt = 0\n                sup_p = False\n                dead = False\n                instant_win = False\n                special_event = None\n\n                for step_i in range(32):\n                    if terrain[cy0][cx0] == \"CRACK\":\n                        if ncr_mut is None:\n                            ncr_mut = set(cracks)\n                        if (cx0, cy0) not in ncr_mut:\n                            ncr_mut.add((cx0, cy0))\n                            step_c += 5.0\n                    if terrain[cy1][cx1] in ONEWAY_DIR:\n                        step_c += 3.5\n\n                    exp_r |= red_mask[cy1][cx1]\n                    exp_b |= blue_mask[cy1][cx1]\n\n                    active_cr = ncr_mut if ncr_mut is not None else cracks\n                    ct = \"PIT\" if ((cx1, cy1) in active_cr and (cx1, cy1) != (cx0, cy0) and terrain[cy1][cx1] == \"CRACK\") else terrain[cy1][cx1]\n                    stop_m = False\n                    if ct == \"PIT\":\n                        dead = True\n                        break\n                    elif ct == \"MIMIC\":\n                        if not allow_damage or nhp <= 45:\n                            dead = True\n cy1)\n                    elif ct == \"HEAL\":\n                        if nhp < 100:\n                            special_event = (\"HEAL\", cx1, cy1)\n                        else:\n  if p_cnt >= 2:\n                            break\n                        ddx, ddy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                        nnx, nny = cx1 + ddx, cy1 + ddy\n                        if not can_h(nnx, nny, cd):\n                            break\n                        if (nnx, nny) in active_cr:\n                            dead = True\n                            break\n nnx, nny = cx1 + ddx, cy1 + ddy\n                        if not can_h(nnx, nny, cd):\n                            break\n                        cx0, cy0, cx1, cy1 = cx1, cy1, nnx, nny\n                    elif ct in MIRROR_MAP:\n                        cd = MIRROR_MAP[ct][cd]\n                        ddx, ddy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                        nnx, nny = cx1 + ddx, cy1 + ddy\n cx1 + kk * ddx, cy1 + kk * ddy\n                            if not _in_bounds(kx, ky) or is_hw(kx, ky) or blk_ow(kx, ky, cd) or occupant[ky][kx] in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                                blk_k = kk\n                                break\n                        if blk_k == 1:\n                            break\n                        tk = (blk_k - 1) if blk_k in (2, 3) else 3\n ncr_mut is not None else cracks\n\n            if has_bombs:\n                dt_b = nturn - t0_eng\n                if any(b[\"timer\"] == dt_b and abs(nx - b[\"x\"]) <= 1 and abs(ny - b[\"y\"]) <= 1 for b in active_bombs):\n                    continue\n\n            ldmg = 0\n            if nturn % 3 == 0 and exp_r:\n                ldmg += 50 * exp_r.bit_count()\n            if nturn % 4 == 0 and exp_b:\n                ldmg += 50 * exp_b.bit_count()\n\n            if ldmg > 0:\n                if not allow_damage or nhp <= ldmg:\n                    continue\n                nhp -= ldmg\n                step_c += 2.5 * ldmg\n\n            ncost = cost + step_c\n\n            if special_event is not None:\n                dominated_ev = False\n                for hj in range(nj, eng[\"jumps\"] + 1):\n                    if special_hits.get((special_event[0], special_event[1], special_event[2], hj), (1e18, None, None))[0] <= ncost + 1e-6:\n                        dominated_ev _trace_stone_from(eng: dict, vx: int, vy: int, dname: str):\n    sx, sy = vx, vy\n    rleft = 6\n    sp = 0\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n    occupant = eng[\"occupant\"]\n    portals = eng[\"portals\"]\n\n    while rleft > 0:\n        dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n        sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n        rleft -= 1\n        if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or _eng_is_high_wall(eng, sx, sy) or occupant[sy][sx] == \"BOX\":\n            return None\n        if occupant[sy][sx] == \"TNT\":\n            return (\"TNT\", sx, sy)\n        t = terrain[sy][sx]\n        if t == \"SWITCH\":\n            return (\"SWITCH\", sx, sy)\n        if t == \"MIMIC\":\n            return (\"MIMIC\", sx, sy)\n        if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n            dname = MIRROR_MAP[t][dname]\n        elif t in           sp += 1\n                if sp == 2:\n                    return None\n    if terrain[sy][sx] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys()):\n        for _ in range(32):\n            dx, dy = DIRECTIONS[dname]\n            sx, sy = sx + dx, sy + dy\n            if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or _eng_is_high_wall(eng, sx, sy) or occupant[sy][sx] == \"BOX\":\n                return None\n            if occupant[sy][sx] == \"TNT\":\n                return (\"TNT\", sx, sy)\n            t = terrain[sy][sx]\n            if t == \"SWITCH\":\n                return (\"SWITCH\", sx, sy)\n            if t == \"MIMIC\":\n                return (\"MIMIC\", sx, sy)\n            if t in MIRROR_MAP:\n                dname = MIRROR_MAP[t][dname]\n            elif t == \"ICE\":\n                continue\n            elif t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and sp < 2:\n                pp = portals.get(PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[t])\n                if pp is not None and occupant[pp[1]][pp[0]] not in (\"BOX\", \"TNT\"):\n                    sx, sy = pp\n                    sp += 1\n                    if sp == 2:\n                        return None\n                else:\n                    return None\n            else:\n                return None\n    return None\n\n\ndef _canonical_hero_anchor(eng: dict):\n    plates_on = _eng_plates_active(eng)\n    gf = eng[\"gates_flipped\"]\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n    occupant = eng[\"occupant\"]\n    sx, sy = eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"]\n    best = (sx, sy)\n    vis = {(sx, sy)}\n    q = collections.deque([(sx, sy)])\n    while q:\n        x, y = q.popleft()\n        if (x, y) < best:\n            best = (x, y)\n        for dx, dy in DIRECTIONS.values():\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if (nx, ny) in vis or not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                continue\n            t = terrain[ny][nx]\n            if t not in (\"EMPTY\", \"PLATE\") and not (t == \"R_GATE\" and gf) and not (t == \"B_GATE\" and not gf) and not (t == \"P_GATE\" and plates_on):\n                continue\n            if occupant[ny][nx] is not None:\n                continue\n            vis.add((nx, ny))\n            q.append((nx, ny))\n    return best\n\n\ndef _world_signature(eng: dict):\n    boxes = []\n    tnts = []\n    cracks_cnt = 0\n    walls_hash = 0\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n    occupant = eng[\"occupant\"]\n    for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n        trow = terrain[y]\n        orow = occupant[y]\n        for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n            o = orow[x]\n            if o == \"BOX\":\n                boxes.append((x, y))\n            elif o == \"TNT\":\n                tnts.append((x, y))\n            t = trow[x]\n            if t == \"CRACK\":\n                cracks_cnt += 1\n            elif t in (\"W\", *ALL_LASER_DIR.keys(), \"MIMIC\", \"HEAL\", \"PIT\"):\n                walls_hash ^= hash((x, y, t))\n    anchor = _canonical_hero_anchor(eng)\n    return (\n        anchor[0], anchor[1],\n        eng[\"has_k1\"], eng[\"has_k2\"], eng[\"gates_flipped\"],\n        eng[\"jumps\"], eng[\"throws\"], eng[\"bombs_inv\"],\n        tuple(boxes), tuple(tnts), cracks_cnt, walls_hash,\n    )\n\n\ndef _is_cell_permanently_box_locked(eng: dict, bx: int, by: int) -> bool:\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n\n    def perm_blk(tx, ty, push_dir, stand_x, stand_y):\n        if not _in_bounds(tx, ty) or not _in_bounds(stand_x, stand_y):\n            return True\n        if terrain[ty][tx] in (\"T_W\", \"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\") or _eng_blocks_oneway(eng, tx, ty, push_dir):\n            return True\n        if terrain[stand_y][stand_x] == \"T_W\" or _eng_blocks_oneway(eng, bx, by, push_dir):\n            return True\n        return False\n\n    h_blk = perm_blk(bx + 1, by, \"R\", bx - 1, by) and perm_blk(bx - 1, by, \"L\", bx + 1, by)\n    v_blk = perm_blk(bx, by + 1, \"D\", bx, by - 1) and perm_blk(bx, by - 1, \"U\", bx, by + 1)\n    return h_blk and v_blk\n\n\ndef _is_box_static_corner_deadlock(eng: dict, bx: int, by: int) -> bool:\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n    if terrain[by][bx] == \"PLATE\":\n        return False\n    for dname, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n        for dist in range(1, 11):\n            nx, ny = bx - dist * dx, by - dist * dy\n            if not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                break\n            t = terrain[ny][nx]\n            if t in ALL_LASER_DIR and ALL_LASER_DIR[t] == dname:\n                return False\n            if t == \"T_W\":\n                break\n    return _is_cell_permanently_box_locked(eng, bx, by)\n\n\ndef _compute_box_push_reach(eng: dict, bx0: int, by0: int) -> set:\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n    portals = eng[\"portals\"]\n    vis = {(bx0, DIRECTIONS.items():\n            sx, sy = x - dx, y - dy\n            nx, ny = x + dx, y + dy\n            if not _in_bounds(sx, sy) or not _in_bounds(nx, ny):\n                continue\n            if terrain[sy][sx] == \"T_W\":\n                continue\n            if terrain[ny][nx] in (\"T_W\", \"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\") or _eng_blocks_oneway(eng, nx, ny, dname):\n                continue\n\n            cx, cy, cd = nx, ny, dname\n            for _ in range(32):\n                if (cx, cy) not in vis:\n                    vis.add((cx, cy))\n                    q.append((cx, cy))\n                   cx, cy = nnx, nny\n                elif ct in MIRROR_MAP:\n                    cd = MIRROR_MAP[ct][cd]\n                    ddx, ddy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                    nnx, nny = cx + ddx, cy + ddy\n                    if not _in_bounds(nnx, nny) or terrain[nny][nnx] in (\"T_W\", \"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\") or _eng_blocks_oneway(eng, nnx, nny, cd):\n                        break\n                    cx, cy = nnx, nny\n                elif ct in SPRING_DIR:\n                    cd = SPRING_DIR[ct]\n                    ddx, ddy = DIRECTIONS[cd]\n                    for kk in (1, _eng_blocks_oneway(eng, kx, ky, cd):\n                            break\n                        if (kx, ky) not in vis:\n                            vis.add((kx, ky))\n                            q.append((kx, ky))\n                    break\n                elif ct in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[ct] in portals:\n                    pp = portals[PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[ct]]\n vis\n\n\ndef _can_ever_win_relaxed(eng: dict, box_reach_cache: dict) -> bool:\n    terrain = eng[\"terrain\"]\n    portals = eng[\"portals\"]\n    has_pgate = any(terrain[y][x] == \"P_GATE\" for y in vis.add(pp)\n                        q.append(pp)\n        return False\n\n    v = tuple(eng[\"targets\"][\"V\"])\n    needs_plates = has_pgate and not reachable_without_pgate(eng[\"hx\"], return False\n        num_boxes = mobile_boxes - plates_still_needing_mobile\n    else:\n        num_boxes = mobile_boxes\n\n    max_total = eng[\"jumps\"] + num_boxes\n\n    def min_res_from(sx, sy, best_r[(nx, ny)] = cand\n                    dq.appendleft((tot_u, box_u, nx, ny))\n                    if t in PORTAL_PAIR_TILE and PORTAL_PAIR_TILE[t] in portals:\n                        pp = pp[0], pp[1]))\n                    elif t in SPRING_DIR:\n                        sdx, sdy = DIRECTIONS[SPRING_DIR[t]]\n                        for kk in (1, 2, 3):\n                            kx, dq.appendleft((tot_u, box_u, kx, ky))\n        return 999, 999\n\n    req = []\n    if not eng[\"has_k1\"]:\n        req.append(tuple(eng[\"targets\"][\"K1\"]))\n    if not eng[\"has_k2\"]:\n num_boxes = 0\n    max_total = eng[\"jumps\"]\n    for g_pt in req + [v]:\n        tr0, _ = min_res_from(eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"], {g_pt})\n        if tr0 > eng[\"jumps\"]:\n            rev_pits = set()\n            rq = collections.deque([g_pt])\n            rvis = {g_pt}\n            while rq:\n                rcx, rcy = rq.popleft()\n                cur_is_pit = (terrain[rcy][rcx] == \"PIT\")\n                for dn, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n                    px, py = rcx - dx, rcy - dy\n                    if not _in_bounds(px, py) or (px, py) in rvis:\n                        continue\n                    if terrain[rcy][rcx] in ONEWAY_DIR and ONEWAY_DIR[terrain[rcy][rcx]] != dn:\n                        continue\n                    pt_t = terrain[py][px]\n                    if pt_t == \"T_W\":\n                        continue\n                    if pt_t == \"PIT\":\n                        rvis.add((px, py))\n                        rev_pits.add((px, py))\n                        rq.append((px, py))\n                    elif not cur_is_pit and pt_t != \"W\":\n                        rvis.add((px, py))\n                        rq.append((px, py))\n            if rev_pits:\n                sink_groups.append(rev_pits)\n\n    num_boxes = saved_nb\n    max_total = eng[\"jumps\"] + num_boxes\n\n    if sink_groups:\n        if len(sink_groups) > len(boxes):\n            return False\n        cand_lists = [\n            [bi for bi, br in enumerate(box_reaches) if br & sset]\n            for sset in sink_groups\n        ]\n        if any(not cl for cl in cand_lists):\n            return False\n        used_b = [False] * len(boxes)\n\n        def match_dfs(idx):\n            if idx == len(cand_lists):\n                return True\n            for bi in cand_lists[idx]:\n                if not used_b[bi]:\n                    used_b[bi] = True\n                    if match_dfs(idx + 1):\n                        return True\n                    used_b[bi] = False\n            return False\n\n        cand_lists.sort(key=len)\n        if not match_dfs(0):\n            return False\n\n    if len(req) == 0:\n        t1, b1 = min_res_from(eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"], {v})\n        return b1 <= num_boxes and (t1 - b1) <= eng[\"jumps\"]\n    elif len(req) == 1:\n        t1, b1 = min_res_from(eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"], {req[0]})\n        if b1 > num_boxes or (t1 - b1) > eng[\"jumps\"]:\n            return False\n        t2, b2 = min_res_from(req[0][0], req[0][1], {v})\n        return max(b1, b2) <= num_boxes and ((t1 - b1) + (t2 - b2)) <= eng[\"jumps\"]\n    else:\n        k1, k2 = req[0], req[1]\n        for first_k, second_k in ((k1, k2), (k2, k1)):\n            t1, b1 = min_res_from(eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"], {first_k})\n            t2, b2 = min_res_from(first_k[0], first_k[1], {second_k})\n            t3, b3 = min_res_from(second_k[0], second_k[1], {v})\n            jtot = (t1 - b1) + (t2 - b2) + (t3 - b3)\n            if max(b1, b2, b3) <= num_boxes and jtot <= eng[\"jumps\"]:\n                return True\n        return False\n\n\ndef _heuristic_v22(eng: dict, has_pgates: bool) -> float:\n    h = 0.0\n    k1 = tuple(eng[\"targets\"][\"K1\"])\n    k2 = tuple(eng[\"targets\"][\"K2\"])\n    v = tuple(eng[\"targets\"][\"V\"])\n    pos = (eng[\"hx\"], eng[\"hy\"])\n\n    if not eng[\"has_k1\"] and not eng[\"has_k2\"]:\n        d_keys = min(\n            _manhattan(pos, k1) + _manhattan(k1, k2) + _manhattan(k2, v),\n            _manhattan(pos, k2) + _manhattan(k2, k1) + _manhattan(k1, v),\n        )\n        h += 75.0 * 2 + 0.9 * d_keys\n    elif not eng[\"has_k1\"]:\n        h += 75.0 + 0.9 * (_manhattan(pos, k1) + _manhattan(k1, v))\n    elif not eng[\"has_k2\"]:\n        h += 75.0 + 0.9 * (_manhattan(pos, k2) + _manhattan(k2, v))\n    else:\n        h += 0.9 * _manhattan(pos, v)\n\n    if has_pgates and eng[\"plates\"]:\n        boxes = [\n            (x, y)\n            for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1)\n            for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1)\n            if eng[\"occupant\"][y][x] == \"BOX\"\n        ]\n        uncovered = [p for p in eng[\"plates\"] if eng[\"occupant\"][p[1]][p[0]] != \"BOX\"]\n        free_boxes = [b for b in boxes if eng[\"terrain\"][b[1]][b[0]] != \"PLATE\"]\n        h += 45.0 * len(uncovered)\n        if uncovered and free_boxes:\n            for up in uncovered:\n                h += 2.5 * min(_manhattan(b, up) for b in free_boxes)\n\n    h += 6.0 * (3 - eng[\"jumps\"]) + 3.0 * (3 - eng[\"throws\"]) + 8.0 * (2 - eng[\"bombs_inv\"])\n    h += 2.5 * (100 - eng[\"hp\"])\n    return h\n\n\ndef _solve_full_dungeon(initial_eng: dict, time_budget: float):\n    \"\"\"\n    Returns (is_complete_win: bool, action_list: list).\n    If time_budget expires on an ultra-huge maze before reaching V, returns the best\n    deadlock-free partial macro prefix so online replanning continues seamlessly!\n    \"\"\"\n    t0 = time.monotonic()\n    deadline = t0 + time_budget\n\n    has_pgates = any(\n        initial_eng[\"terrain\"][y][x] == \"P_GATE\"\n        for y in range(GRID_HEIGHT)\n        for x in range(GRID_WIDTH)\n    )\n    num_plates = len(initial_eng[\"plates\"])\n    box_reach_cache = {}\n    relax_memo = {}\n\n    best_partial = []\n    best_partial_rank = (\n        int(initial_eng[\"has_k1\"]) + int(initial_eng[\"has_k2\"]),\n        sum(1 for px, py in initial_eng[\"plates\"] if initial_eng[\"occupant\"][py][px] == \"BOX\"),\n        -_heuristic_v22(initial_eng, has_pgates),\n    )\n\n    for allow_dmg in (False, True):\n        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:\n            break\n        sub_deadline = deadline if allow_dmg else (t0 + time_budget * 0.80)\n\n        uid = 0\n        h0 = _heuristic_v22(initial_eng, has_pgates)\n        pq = [(h0, 0.0, uid, initial_eng, ())]\n        best_g = {_world_signature(initial_eng): 0.0}\n\n        while pq and time.monotonic() < sub_deadline:\n            _, g_val, _, eng, macro_path = heapq.heappop(pq)\n            sig = _world_signature(eng)\n            if g_val > best_g.get(sig, 1e18) + 1e-6:\n                continue\n\n            if macro_path:\n                prank = (\n                    int(eng[\"has_k1\"]) + int(eng[\"has_k2\"]),\n                    sum(1 for px, py in eng[\"plates\"] if eng[\"occupant\"][py][px] == \"BOX\"),\n                    -_heuristic_v22(eng, has_pgates),\n                )\n                if prank > best_partial_rank:\n                    best_partial_rank = prank\n                    best_partial = list(macro_path)\n\n            best_at_cell, reconstruct, special_hits, win_nav = _fast_hero_nav(eng, allow_damage=allow_dmg)\n            if win_nav is not None:\n                full_sol = list(macro_path + win_nav)\n                v_eng = _clone_eng(initial_eng)\n                for a in full_sol:\n                    _eng_step(v_eng, a)\n                if v_eng[\"status\"] == \"CLEARED\":\n                    return True, full_sol\n\n            def push_child(nav_acts, extra_acts, extra_penalty=0.0):\n                nonlocal uid, best_partial, best_partial_rank\n                tot_acts = macro_path + nav_acts + extra_acts\n                if len(tot_acts) > MAX_TURNS:\n                    return None\n                c_eng = _clone_eng(eng)\n                hp_before = c_eng[\"hp\"]\n                for a in nav_acts + extra_acts:\n                    _eng_step(c_eng, a)\n                    if c_eng[\"status\"] in (\"DEAD\", \"CLEARED\"):\n                        break\n                if c_eng[\"status\"] == \"DEAD\":\n                    return None\n                if not allow_dmg and c_eng[\"hp\"] < hp_before:\n                    return None\n                if c_eng[\"status\"] == \"CLEARED\":\n                    return list(tot_acts)\n\n                if (\n                    c_eng[\"jumps\"] < eng[\"jumps\"]\n                    or c_eng[\"has_k1\"] != eng[\"has_k1\"]\n                    or c_eng[\"has_k2\"] != eng[\"has_k2\"]\n                    or any(a.startswith(\"MOVE_\") for a in extra_acts)\n                    or not has_pgates\n                ):\n                    rm_key = (\n                        _canonical_hero_anchor(c_eng), c_eng[\"has_k1\"], c_eng[\"has_k2\"], c_eng[\"jumps\"],\n                        tuple((x, y) for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1) for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1) if c_eng[\"occupant\"][y][x] == \"BOX\"),\n                        sum(1 for y in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1) for x in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1) if c_eng[\"terrain\"][y][x] == \"PIT\"),\n                    )\n                    ok_rel = relax_memo.get(rm_key)\n                    if ok_rel is None:\n                        ok_rel = _can_ever_win_relaxed(c_eng, box_reach_cache)\n                        relax_memo[rm_key] = ok_rel\n                    if not ok_rel:\n                        return None\n\n                if has_pgates and not _eng_plates_active(c_eng):\n                    usable_boxes = 0\n                    for by in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                        for bx in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                            if c_eng[\"occupant\"][by][bx] == \"BOX\" and not _is_box_static_corner_deadlock(c_eng, bx, by):\n                                usable_boxes += 1\n                    if usable_boxes < num_plates:\n                        return None\n\n                new_g = float(c_eng[\"turn\"]) + 2.5 * (100 - c_eng[\"hp\"]) + extra_penalty\n                nsig = _world_signature(c_eng)\n                if new_g < best_g.get(nsig, 1e18):\n                    best_g[nsig] = new_g\n                    uid += 1\n                    nh = _heuristic_v22(c_eng, has_pgates)\n                    heapq.heappush(pq, (new_g + 1.35 * nh, new_g, uid, c_eng, tot_acts))\n                return None\n\n            # 1. 拾取钥匙 / 踩开关 / 吃血包 / 受控踩怪\n            for (ekind, ex, ey, _ej), (_, pnid, lact) in special_hits.items():\n                if ekind == \"HEAL\" and eng[\"hp\"] == 100:\n                    continue\n                win_sol = push_child(reconstruct(pnid, lact), ())\n                if win_sol is not None:\n                    return True, win_sol\n\n            # 2. 推箱子宏动作\n            for by in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                for bx in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                    if eng[\"occupant\"][by][bx] != \"BOX\":\n                        continue\n                    for dname, (dx, dy) in DIRECTIONS.items():\n                        sx, sy = bx - dx, by - dy\n                        p2x, p2y = bx + dx, by + dy\n                        if (sx, sy) not in best_at_cell:\n                            continue\n                        if _eng_is_high_wall(eng, bx, by) or _eng_blocks_oneway(eng, bx, by, dname):\n                            continue\n                        if not _in_bounds(p2x, p2y) or _eng_is_high_wall(eng, p2x, p2y) or _eng_blocks_oneway(eng, p2x, p2y, dname):\n                            continue\n                        if eng[\"occupant\"][p2y][p2x] is not None or eng[\"terrain\"][p2y][p2x] in (\"K1\", \"K2\", \"V\"):\n                            continue\n                        _, nid, _ = best_at_cell[(sx, sy)]\n                        win_sol = push_child(reconstruct(nid), (\"MOVE_\" + dname,))\n                        if win_sol is not None:\n                            return True, win_sol\n\n            # 3. 远程飞石打靶 (支持单向门穿透、折跃镜反弹与第6格冰滑)\n            walkable_switches = {(ex, ey) for (ekind, ex, ey, _ej) in special_hits if ekind == \"SWITCH\"}\n            if eng[\"throws\"] > 0:\n                interest_rows = set()\n                interest_cols = set()\n                for ty_i in range(1, GRID_HEIGHT - 1):\n                    trow = eng[\"terrain\"][ty_i]\n                    orow = eng[\"occupant\"][ty_i]\n                    for tx_i in range(1, GRID_WIDTH - 1):\n                        tt = trow[tx_i]\n                        if orow[tx_i] == \"TNT\" or tt in (\"MIMIC\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys(), *PORTAL_PAIR_TILE.keys()) or (tt == \"SWITCH\" and (tx_i, ty_i) not in walkable_switches):\n                            interest_rows.add(ty_i)\n                            interest_cols.add(tx_i)\n                best_throws = {}\n                if interest_rows:\n                    for (vx, vy), (rcost, nid, _rj) in best_at_cell.items():\n                        if vy not in interest_rows and vx not in interest_cols:\n                            continue\n                        if eng[\"terrain\"][vy][vx] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys(), *SPRING_DIR.keys(), *PORTAL_PAIR_TILE.keys()):\n                            continue\n                        for dname in (\"U\", \"D\", \"L\", \"R\"):\n                            if dname in (\"L\", \"R\") and vy not in interest_rows:\n                                continue\n                            if dname in (\"U\", \"D\") and vx not in interest_cols:\n                                continue\n                            hit = _trace_stone_from(eng, vx, vy, dname)\n                            if hit is None:\n                                continue\n                            hkind, tx, ty = hit\n                            if hkind == \"SWITCH\" and (tx, ty) in walkable_switches:\n                                continue\n                            if hkind == \"TNT\" and abs(vx - tx) <= 1 and abs(vy - ty) <= 1:\n                                continue\n                            tkey = (hkind, tx, ty)\n                            if rcost < best_throws.get(tkey, (1e18, None, None))[0]:\n                                best_throws[tkey] = (rcost, nid, \"THROW_\" + dname)\n                for _, (_, nid, tact) in best_throws.items():\n                    win_sol = push_child(reconstruct(nid), (tact,), extra_penalty=18.0)\n                    if win_sol is not None:\n                        return True, win_sol\n\n            # 4. 拓扑割墙炸弹破障宏动作 (含 3 回合安全闪避捆绑验证)\n            if eng[\"bombs_inv\"] > 0 and not eng[\"bombs\"]:\n                no_jump_cells = {pos for pos, (_, _, rj) in best_at_cell.items() if rj == eng[\"jumps\"]}\n                bomb_cands = []\n                active_lms = []\n                if not eng[\"has_k1\"]:\n                    active_lms.append(eng[\"targets\"][\"K1\"])\n                if not eng[\"has_k2\"]:\n                    active_lms.append(eng[\"targets\"][\"K2\"])\n                if eng[\"has_k1\"] and eng[\"has_k2\"]:\n                    active_lms.append(eng[\"targets\"][\"V\"])\n\n                for (bx, by), (rcost, nid, _rj) in best_at_cell.items():\n                    if _rj < eng[\"jumps\"]:\n                        continue\n                    if eng[\"terrain\"][by][bx] in (\"ICE\", *MIRROR_MAP.keys(), *SPRING_DIR.keys(), *PORTAL_PAIR_TILE.keys()):\n                        continue\n                    opens_new_space = False\n                    crater_breaches_room = False\n                    destroys_laser = False\n                    near_active_target = 999\n\n                    blast_set = {\n                        (bx + dx, by + dy)\n                        for dy in (-1, 0, 1)\n                        for dx in (-1, 0, 1)\n                        if _in_bounds(bx + dx, by + dy) and eng[\"terrain\"][by + dy][bx + dx] != \"T_W\"\n                    }\n                    for wx, wy in blast_set:\n                        wt = eng[\"terrain\"][wy][wx]\n                        wo = eng[\"occupant\"][wy][wx]\n                        if wt in ALL_LASER_DIR:\n                            destroys_laser = True\n                        if wt in DESTRUCTIBLE_WALLS or wo == \"TNT\":\n                            for tlm in active_lms:\n                                near_active_target = min(near_active_target, _manhattan((wx, wy), tlm))\n                            for adx, ady in DIRECTIONS.values():\n                                ux, uy = wx + adx, wy + ady\n                                if _in_bounds(ux, uy) and (ux, uy) not in no_jump_cells:\n                                    ut = eng[\"terrain\"][uy][ux]\n                                    if not _eng_is_high_wall(eng, ux, uy) and ut != \"PIT\" and eng[\"occupant\"][uy][ux] != \"TNT\":\n                                        opens_new_space = True\n                                        if (ux, uy) not in blast_set:\n                                            crater_breaches_room = True\n                                    elif eng[\"bombs_inv\"] >= 2 and ut in DESTRUCTIBLE_WALLS:\n                                        for bdx, bdy in DIRECTIONS.values():\n                                            u2x, u2y = ux + bdx, uy + bdy\n                                            if _in_bounds(u2x, u2y) and (u2x, u2y) not in no_jump_cells and not _eng_is_high_wall(eng, u2x, u2y) and eng[\"terrain\"][u2y][u2x] != \"PIT\":\n                                                opens_new_space = True\n                                                break\n                    if opens_new_space or (allow_dmg and destroys_laser):\n                        bomb_cands.append((0 if crater_breaches_room else 1, near_active_target, rcost, bx, by, nid))\n\n                by_target = sorted(bomb_cands, key=lambda it: (it[0], it[1], it[2]))\n                by_local = sorted(bomb_cands, key=lambda it: (it[0], it[2], it[1]))\n                dedup_bombs = []\n                for pool, cap in ((by_target, 4), (by_local, 7)):\n                    for item in pool:\n                        _, _, _, bx, by, _ = item\n                        if all(_manhattan((bx, by), (ox, oy)) >= 2 for _, _, _, ox, oy, _ in dedup_bombs):\n                            dedup_bombs.append(item)\n                            if len(dedup_bombs) >= cap:\n                                break\n                for cb_flag, _, _, bx, by, nid in dedup_bombs:\n                    if time.monotonic() >= sub_deadline:\n                        break\n                    npath = reconstruct(nid)\n                    b_eng = _clone_eng(eng)\n                    ok_nav = True\n                    for a in npath:\n                        _eng_step(b_eng, a)\n                        if b_eng[\"status\"] != \"ALIVE\" or (not allow_dmg and b_eng[\"hp\"] < eng[\"hp\"]):\n                            ok_nav = False\n                            break\n                    if not ok_nav or (b_eng[\"hx\"], b_eng[\"hy\"]) != (bx, by):\n                        continue\n                    _eng_step(b_eng, \"BOMB\")\n                    if b_eng[\"status\"] != \"ALIVE\" or (not allow_dmg and b_eng[\"hp\"] < eng[\"hp\"]):\n                        continue\n                    found_dodge = None\n                    dodge_moves = (\"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_L\", \"MOVE_R\", \"WAIT\", \"JUMP_U\", \"JUMP_D\", \"JUMP_L\", \"JUMP_R\")\n                    for d1 in dodge_moves:\n                        if d1.startswith(\"JUMP_\") and b_eng[\"jumps\"] <= 0:\n                            continue\n                        e1 = _clone_eng(b_eng)\n                        _eng_step(e1, d1)\n                        if e1[\"status\"] == \"CLEARED\":\n                            return True, list(macro_path + npath + (\"BOMB\", d1))\n                        if e1[\"status\"] != \"ALIVE\" or (not allow_dmg and e1[\"hp\"] < eng[\"hp\"]):\n                            continue\n                        for d2 in dodge_moves:\n                            if d2.startswith(\"JUMP_\") and e1[\"jumps\"] <= 0:\n                                continue\n                            e2 = _clone_eng(e1)\n                            _eng_step(e2, d2)\n                            if e2[\"status\"] == \"CLEARED\":\n                                return True, list(macro_path + npath + (\"BOMB\", d1, d2))\n                            if e2[\"status\"] == \"ALIVE\" and (allow_dmg or e2[\"hp\"] == eng[\"hp\"]):\n                                found_dodge = (\"BOMB\", d1, d2)\n                                break\n                        if found_dodge is not None:\n                            break\n                    if found_dodge is not None:\n                        win_sol = push_child(npath, found_dodge, extra_penalty=(32.0 if cb_flag == 0 else 65.0))\n                        if win_sol is not None:\n                            return True, win_sol\n\n    return False, best_partial\n\n\ndef _emergency_safe_step(eng: dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"当全局搜索未产出序列时的毫秒级 2-ply 安全贪心兜底，保证绝不接口报错或原地受死。\"\"\"\n    k1 = tuple(eng[\"targets\"][\"K1\"])\n    k2 = tuple(eng[\"targets\"][\"K2\"])\n    v = tuple(eng[\"targets\"][\"V\"])\n    goal = v if (eng[\"has_k1\"] and eng[\"has_k2\"]) else (k2 if eng[\"has_k1\"] else k1)\n\n    best_act = \"WAIT\"\n    best_sc = (-1, -9999, -9999)\n    cand = (\"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_L\", \"MOVE_R\", \"WAIT\", \"JUMP_U\", \"JUMP_D\", \"JUMP_L\", \"JUMP_R\")\n    for act in cand:\n        if act.startswith(\"JUMP_\") and eng[\"jumps\"] <= 0:\n            continue\n        e1 = _clone_eng(eng)\n        _eng_step(e1, act)\n        if e1[\"status\"] == \"CLEARED\":\n            return act\n        if e1[\"status\"] != \"ALIVE\":\n            continue\n        survives_2 = 0\n        for act2 in (\"WAIT\", \"MOVE_U\", \"MOVE_D\", \"MOVE_L\", \"MOVE_R\"):\n            e2 = _clone_eng(e1)\n            _eng_step(e2, act2)\n            if e2[\"status\"] in (\"ALIVE\", \"CLEARED\") and e2[\"hp\"] == e1[\"hp\"]:\n                survives_2 = 1\n                break\n        sc = (survives_2, e1[\"hp\"], -_manhattan((e1[\"hx\"], e1[\"hy\"]), goal))\n        if sc > best_sc:\n            best_sc = sc\n            best_act = act\n    return best_act\n\n\ndef _state_quick_sig(state: dict) -> tuple:\n    h = state[\"hero\"]\n    return (\n        h[\"x\"], h[\"y\"], h[\"hp\"],\n        h[\"keys\"][0], h[\"keys\"][1],\n        state[\"gates_flipped\"], state.get(\"plates_active\", False),\n    )\n\n\ndef _store_plan_in_memory(sim_eng: dict, plan: list, is_complete: bool, memory: dict) -> None:\n    expected_sigs = []\n    probe = _clone_eng(sim_eng)\n    for act in plan:\n        expected_sigs.append((\n            probe[\"hx\"], probe[\"hy\"], probe[\"hp\"],\n            probe[\"has_k1\"], probe[\"has_k2\"],\n            probe[\"gates_flipped\"], _eng_plates_active(probe),\n        ))\n        _eng_step(probe, act)\n    memory[\"plan\"] = plan\n    memory[\"sigs\"] = expected_sigs\n    memory[\"idx\"] = 0\n    memory[\"complete\"] = is_complete\n\n\ndef decide_action(state: dict, memory: dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    v2.2 FULL 明牌全局规划决策器：\n    - Turn 1 利用 1.58s 安全窗口执行完整宏动作搜索并缓存精确校验轨迹，Turn 2..240 0.02ms 极速出招；\n    - 若遇超深地图分段求解或子进程重启导致 memory 清空，自动按 0.32s 安全预算在线续算无缝衔接。\n    \"\"\"\n    turn = state[\"turn\"]\n    budget = 1.58 if turn == 1 else 0.32\n\n    # 1. 若已有预计算的完整/分段动作序列，先做精确状态指纹校验后直接 O(1) 执行\n    if \"plan\" in memory and memory[\"idx\"] I'm currently implementing the Hero & Demon upgrades in `submission.py`. I've added the decoy box placements as planned and adjusted the unreachable "T_W" tiles to reduce gold costs. Specifically, I've integrated `true_pit_targets` computation, optimizing heuristics in `_solve_full_dungeon`. Further, I'm integrating the proposed direct box-line/corner chaining and adding deadline checks in the box-pushing macro to improve box-pushing efficiency.


**Optimizing Macro Jumps**

I'm now focusing on optimizing macro jumps. The core idea involves pushing a box to a sink (like a `PLATE` or `PIT`) in a single macro jump, which I've observed as a significant performance booster. For instance, pushing a box to a plate or pit now happens in a single jump, which, as demonstrated in `BENCH_STRONG`, brings the solve time down dramatically. I'm also passing `forbidden_sigs` from `memory["visited_sigs"]` to `_solve_full_dungeon` to prevent replanning visited states. Lastly, I'm setting a turn >= 2 budget of 0.24 seconds with checks to stay under 50% of the limit.

**Analyzing Performance Drops**

I'm seeing a significant performance drop in `[OPP_Standard_Macro_AStar]` on the new 6-box demon map. It went from cleared to a timeout, losing a lot of points. The 6-box Sokoban black hole seems to be the culprit. I'm focusing on why this happened specifically on this map.


**Revising Unnecessary Actions**

I've discovered the root cause of the slowdown! My hero solver was unnecessarily pushing decoy boxes (`B5`, `B6`) around, even after plates and pits had already been assigned to closer boxes. This happened even when all required basins were filled! I need to implement a condition to prevent such pointless movements.


**Refining Macro Action Criteria**

I'm now zeroing in on the macro action generation. It's clear that the existing logic iterates through box push candidates (`B5`, `B6`) without proper regard for their proximity to sinks. The sorting criteria need adjustment. Specifically, I must prioritize pushes that demonstrably reduce the minimum distance to any sink. It's absurd that useless decoys get equal consideration.



**Confirming Optimization Benefits**

I've tested the new modifications on several scenarios. The changes I've made, which focus on pushing boxes toward sinks using refined criteria, including prioritizing sink-approaching pushes and capping useless pushes, have proven highly effective. On `CARBON_Hero_vs_Own`, I can now clear my 6-box black hole map in under 400ms, completely ignoring the decoy boxes. The same logic allows me to clear the `TORTURE_6BOX_4PLATE` configuration swiftly. I must now verify the impact on the `CARBON_V22_DEMON` score.


**Investigating Cost Imbalance**

I'm now staring at the cost breakdown. It appears I've overspent on "T_W" tiles, specifically in the dungeon building phase. This is because the score calculation in my earlier timeout used too much game cost to get a win! The biggest issue is that 1,250 G was spent on only 50 "T_W" tiles! I need to find the specific tile costs and compare them. I'm checking the `build_dungeon()` function to trace this usage.


**Optimizing Tile Usage**

I've hit a breakthrough! I realized I can drastically reduce "T_W" tile usage in the dungeon. By repositioning `K2` and shrinking the South-West basin against the outer free `y=25` space, I can cut the tile count substantially. This will save a significant amount of gold! I need to implement these spatial shifts to save some gold!


**Evaluating Tile Replacement**

I'm now examining how to replace "T_W" tiles in the `build_dungeon()` function. I can save a lot of gold by replacing them with "W" tiles without affecting the solution. Specifically, I see a substantial saving by replacing the horizontal line at y=15 with a thinner "W" line, even though it will not change the end state. I'm also considering other "W" tiles near B2, B4, Switch and Vault.

**Analyzing the Logic Bug**

I've pinpointed a crucial line in `submission.py` under the comment `# 2. 推箱子宏动作`. This line, specifically `d_prev = min((_manhattan((bx, by), sk) for sk in`, seems to be the culprit. It appears to be expanding `B5` and `B6` prematurely, before the `plates_active == True` condition is fully engaged. The logic within this line merits closer inspection.


**Refining the Reachability**

I've been examining the reachability logic, and discovered a significant issue. Specifically, the premature expansion of boxes `B5` and `B6` occurs before the `plates_active == True` condition is fully engaged. I've realized that the Manhattan distance calculation doesn't accurately reflect the constraints in some cases. My fix involves a per-sink assignment method.


**Revising Reachable Boxes**

I've made a crucial refinement. It's now clear that to improve our approach, we MUST consider two critical factors. First, boxes need to be among the two closest reachable free boxes to a sink. Then, I need the logic to calculate reachability to include a check such as `sk in box_reach_cache[(bx, by)]`. I have found that this logic change fixes an important error!


**Optimizing Wall Configurations**

I'm now focusing on optimizing the wall configurations to reduce the overall cost. I've identified several areas where "T_W" tiles can be replaced with "W" tiles without affecting the solution's validity. Specifically, I see an easy 350G save by changing several tiles at y=15 to `"W"` which I am now confirming. Further, there is also 20G in potential savings in the K2 area.
